        function M:SafeCallback(w, ...)
            if (not w) then
                return
            end
            if not const_0.NotifyOnError then
                return w(...)
            end
            local z, i = pcall(w, ...)
            if not z then
                local w, w = i:find(":%d+: ")
                if not w then
                    return const_0:Notify(i)
                end
                return const_0:Notify(i:sub(w + 1), 3)
            end
        end

        function M:RemoveFromRegistry(w)
            local z = M.RegistryMap[w]
            if z then
                for o = #const_0.Registry, 1, -1 do
                    if const_0.Registry[o] == z then
                        table.remove(const_0.Registry, o)
                    end
                end
                for o = #const_0.HudRegistry, 1, -1 do
                    if const_0.HudRegistry[o] == z then
                        table.remove(const_0.HudRegistry, o)
                    end
                end
                const_0.RegistryMap[w] = nil
            end
        end

                Q.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            end
            local Z = {}
            local l = M:Create(a[3][a[2]], { AnchorPoint = Q.AnchorPoint, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = Q.Position, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Q.Size.X, Q.Size.Y), Visible = Q.AutoShow, ZIndex = 1, Parent = N })
            if (not Q.RemoveTopbar) then
                const_0:MakeDraggableOutline(l, 25)
            end
            local N = M:Create(a[3][a[2]], { BackgroundColor3 = M.MainColor, BorderColor3 = M.OutlineColor, BorderMode = Enum.BorderMode.Inset, Position = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -2), ZIndex = 1, Parent = l })
            const_0:AddToRegistry(N, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor", BorderColor3 = const_15[3][const_15[2]] })
            if (not Q.RemoveTopbar) then
                local P = M:CreateLabel({ Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 25), Text = Q.Title or "", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, ZIndex = 1, Font = M.Font, Parent = N })
                local P = M:Create("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = M.Font, TextColor3 = M.AccentColor, TextSize = 16, TextStrokeTransparency = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, -8, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 25), Text = Q.Version or "", RichText = true, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, ZIndex = 1, Parent = N })
                const_0:AddToRegistry(P, { TextColor3 = "AccentColor" })
            end
            local P = Q.RemoveTopbar and 8 or 25
            local b = Q.RemoveTopbar and -16 or -33
            local V = M:Create(a[3][a[2]], { BackgroundColor3 = M.BackgroundColor, BorderColor3 = M.OutlineColor, Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, P), Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, b), ZIndex = 1, Parent = N })
            const_0:AddToRegistry(V, { BackgroundColor3 = "BackgroundColor", BorderColor3 = const_15[3][const_15[2]] })
            local N = M:Create(a[3][a[2]], { BackgroundColor3 = M.BackgroundColor, BorderColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BorderMode = Enum.BorderMode.Inset, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), ZIndex = 1, Parent = V })
            const_0:AddToRegistry(N, { BackgroundColor3 = "BackgroundColor" })
            local P = M:Create(a[3][a[2]], { BackgroundColor3 = M.BackgroundColor, BorderColor3 = M.OutlineColor, Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8), Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -16), ZIndex = 2, Visible = true, Parent = N })
            local N = M:Create(a[3][a[2]], { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = P, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2) })
            const_0:Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 0), FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left, Parent = N })
            const_0:AddToRegistry(P, { BackgroundColor3 = "BackgroundColor", BorderColor3 = const_15[3][const_15[2]] })
            const_0:AddToRegistry(N, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor", BorderColor3 = const_15[3][const_15[2]] })
            Z.Holder = l
            Z.Container = N
            function Z:Resize()
                local P = 0
                for b, b in next, N:GetChildren() do
                    if (not b:IsA("UIListLayout")) and b.Visible then
                        P = P + b.AbsoluteSize.Y
                    end
                end
                l.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Q.Size.X, 16 + P + (N.AbsolutePosition.Y - l.AbsolutePosition.Y))
            end
            function Z:Toggle()
                l.Visible = not l.Visible
            end
            function Z:GetSize()
                return N.AbsoluteSize
            end
            N:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
                task.wait()
                Z:Resize()
            end)
            Z:Resize()
            setmetatable(Z, const_14)
            return Z
        end
        local Z, Q, N = UDim2.new, ColorSequence.new, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new
        function M:Notify(l, P)
            local j, b = M:GetTextBounds(l, M.Font, 14)
            b = b + 7
            local V = S.Transparency
            local u, a, _, A = i()
            local i = T:Clone()
            i.BackgroundColor3 = u
            i.Name = const_11(256 - const_17(1, #l % 256))
            i.Size = Z(0, 0, 0, b)
            i.Transparency = V
            local z = i.inner
            z.BackgroundColor3 = u
            z.BorderColor3 = _
            z.Transparency = V
            local o = z.inner
            o.Transparency = V
            local z = o.UIGradient
            z.Color = Q({ N(0, const_0:GetDarkerColor(u)), N(1, u) })
            local z = o.label
            z.Text = l
            z.TextColor3 = A
            local z = i.bar
            z.BackgroundColor3 = a
            z.Size = w.BarSize[const_9.BarSide] or Z(0, 3, 1, 2)
            z.Position = w.BarPosition[const_9.BarSide]
            i.Parent = const_0.NotificationArea
            pcall(i.TweenSize, i, UDim2.new(0, j + 8 + 4, 0, b), "Out", "Quad", 0.4, true)
            task.spawn(function()
                wait(P or 5)
                pcall(i.TweenSize, i, UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, b), "Out", "Quad", 0.4, true)
                wait(0.4)
                i:Destroy()
            end)
        end
        function M:IsVisible()
            return const_7[3][const_7[2]]
        end
        const_0:CreateEvent("VisibilityChanged")
    end
end

local ui_toggle_module = function(constants)
    local const_10 = constants[10]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_6 = constants[6]
    local const_12 = constants[12]
    local const_11 = constants[11]
    local const_13 = constants[13]
    local const_9 = constants[9]
    local const_7 = constants[7]
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_14 = constants[14]
    local const_8 = constants[8]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    return function()
        function u:Display()
            const_2.BackgroundColor3 = const_0.Value and const_3.AccentColor or const_3.MainColor
            const_2.BorderColor3 = const_0.Value and const_3.AccentColorDark or const_3.OutlineColor
            const_3.RegistryMap[const_2].Properties.BackgroundColor3 = const_0.Value and "AccentColor" or "MainColor"
            const_3.RegistryMap[const_2].Properties.BorderColor3 = const_0.Value and "AccentColorDark" or const_13[3][const_13[2]]
        end
        const_0.UpdateColors = const_0.Display
        function u:OnChanged(w)
            table.insert(const_0.ChangedFuncs, w)
            w(const_0.Value)
        end
        function u:SetValue(w)
            if (const_0.SettingValue) then
                return
            end
            const_0.SettingValue = true
            w = (not (not w))
            const_0.Value = w
            const_0:Display()
            for i, i in next, const_0.Addons do
                if i.Type == "KeyPicker" and i.SyncToggleState then
                    i.Toggled = w
                    i:Update()
                end
            end
            const_3:SafeCallback(const_0.Callback, w)
            for i, i in const_0.ChangedFuncs do
                const_3:SafeCallback(i, w)
            end
            const_3:UpdateDependencyBoxes()
            const_0.SettingValue = false
        end
        function u:SetVisible(w)
            const_0.Visible = w
            const_4.Visible = const_0.Visible
            if Blank then
                Blank.Visible = const_0.Visible
            end
            const_1:Resize()
        end
        function u:Remove()
            for w, w in const_7 do
                w:Destroy()
            end
            const_9[const_5] = nil
            const_6:Destroy()
            table.clear(const_0)
            const_1:Resize()
        end
        const_10.InputBegan:Connect(function(w)
            if (const_3.IsDragging) then
                return
            end
            if (w.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and not const_3:MouseIsOverOpenedFrame()) or w.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                const_0:SetValue(not const_0.Value)
                const_3:AttemptSave()
            end
        end)
        if (const_3.FlagCopying) then
            local w = P:AddContextMenu(j, S)
            w:AddOption("Copy Flag", function()
                pcall(setclipboard, const_0.Idx)
                task.wait()
                const_3:Notify("Copied flag to clipboard!", 2)
                w:Hide()
            end)
        end
        if const_0.Risky then
            const_3:RemoveFromRegistry(const_8)
            const_8.TextColor3 = const_3.RiskColor
            const_3:AddToRegistry(const_8, { TextColor3 = "RiskColor" })
        end
        const_0:Display()
        table.insert(const_7, const_1:AddBlank(const_11.BlankSize or 7))
        const_1:Resize()
        const_0.ColorPickerCount = 0
        const_0.ToggleRegion = const_10
        const_0.TextLabel = const_8
        const_0.Container = const_12
        setmetatable(const_0, const_14)
        const_9[const_5] = const_0
        const_3:UpdateDependencyBoxes()
    end
end

local ui_slider_module = function(constants)
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_6 = constants[6]
    local const_11 = constants[11]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_8 = constants[8]
    local const_16 = constants[16]
    local const_10 = constants[10]
    local const_7 = constants[7]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_13 = constants[13]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_12 = constants[12]
    local const_15 = constants[15]
    local const_17 = constants[17]
    local const_14 = constants[14]
    local const_9 = constants[9]
    return function()
        function j:UpdateColors()
            const_3.BackgroundColor3 = const_7.AccentColor
            const_3.BorderColor3 = const_7.AccentColorDark
        end
        function j:Display()
            local w = S.Suffix or ""
            if const_4.Compact then
                const_10.Text = const_4.Text .. ": " .. const_1.Value .. w
            elseif const_4.HideMax then
                const_10.Text = string.format("%s", const_1.Value .. w)
            else
                const_10.Text = string.format("%s/%s", const_1.Value .. w, const_1.Max .. w)
            end
            local w = N:MapValue(j.Value, j.Min, j.Max, 0, 1)
            if const_5[3][const_5[2]] then
                const_5[3][const_5[2]]:Cancel()
            end
            const_5[3][const_5[2]] = const_9:Create(const_3, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Size = UDim2.new(w, 0, 1, 0) })
            const_5[3][const_5[2]]:Play()
            const_8.Visible = not (w == 1 or w == 0)
        end
        function j:OnChanged(w)
            table.insert(const_1.ChangedFuncs, w)
            w(const_1.Value)
        end
        local function w(i)
            if (const_1.Increment) then
                local Q = math.round(i / j.Increment) * j.Increment
                return tonumber(string.format("%.10f", Q))
            elseif const_1.Rounding == 0 then
                return math.floor(i)
            end
            return tonumber(string.format("%." .. const_1.Rounding .. "f", i))
        end
        function j:GetValueFromXOffset(i)
            return w(const_7:MapValue(i, 0, const_1.MaxSize, const_1.Min, const_1.Max)) + 0
        end
        function j:Remove()
            for w, w in const_15 do
                w:Destroy()
            end
            const_14[const_13] = nil
            const_1.HolderFrame:Destroy()
            table.clear(const_1)
            const_0:Resize()
        end
        function j:SetValue(w)
            local i = tonumber(w)
            if (not i) then
                return
            end
            i = math.clamp(i, const_1.Min, const_1.Max)
            const_1.Value = i
            const_1:Display()
            const_7:SafeCallback(const_1.Callback, const_1.Value)
            for w, w in self.ChangedFuncs do
                const_7:SafeCallback(w, i)
            end
        end
        function j:AddSlider(w, i)
            return const_11.AddSlider(const_0, w, i, const_1)
        end
        const_12.InputBegan:Connect(function(w)
            if (const_7.IsDragging) then
                return
            end
            if (w.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and not const_7:MouseIsOverOpenedFrame()) then
                local Z = M.X
                local i = z.Size.X.Offset
                local Q = Z - (z.AbsolutePosition.X + i)
                while (const_16:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)) do
                    local T = M.X
                    local M = math.clamp(i + (T - Z) + Q, 0, j.MaxSize)
                    local Z = j:GetValueFromXOffset(M)
                    local M = j.Value
                    const_1.Value = Z
                    const_1:Display()
                    if (Z ~= M) then
                        const_7:SafeCallback(const_1.Callback, const_1.Value)
                        for M, M in const_1.ChangedFuncs do
                            const_7:SafeCallback(M, Z)
                        end
                    end
                    const_17:Wait()
                end
                const_7:AttemptSave()
            end
            if (w.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and not const_7:MouseIsOverOpenedFrame()) then
                local M = w.Position.X
                local Z = z.Size.X.Offset
                local i = M - (z.AbsolutePosition.X + Z)
                local z = true
                local T
                T = w.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if (w.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End) then
                        z = false
                    end
                end)
                while (z) do
                    local z = w.Position.X
                    local w = math.clamp(Z + (z - M) + i, 0, j.MaxSize)
                    local z = j:GetValueFromXOffset(w)
                    local w = j.Value
                    const_1.Value = z
                    const_1:Display()
                    if (z ~= w) then
                        const_7:SafeCallback(const_1.Callback, const_1.Value)
                        for w, w in const_1.ChangedFuncs do
                            const_7:SafeCallback(w, z)
                        end
                    end
                    const_17:Wait()
                end
                if (T) then
                    T:Disconnect()
                end
                const_7:AttemptSave()
            end
        end)
        if (const_7.FlagCopying) then
            local w = N:AddContextMenu(V)
            w:AddOption("Copy Flag", function()
                pcall(setclipboard, const_1.Idx)
                task.wait()
                const_7:Notify("Copied flag to clipboard!", 2)
                w:Hide()
            end)
        end
        const_1:Display()
        if (not const_2) then
            table.insert(const_15, const_0:AddBlank(const_4.BlankSize or 6))
            const_0:Resize()
        end
        const_14[const_13] = const_1
    end
end

local ui_keybind_module = function(constants)
    local const_7 = constants[7]
    local const_9 = constants[9]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_8 = constants[8]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_6 = constants[6]
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_10 = constants[10]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    return function()
        local w = M.Modes or { "Always", "Toggle", "Hold" }
        local j = N:AddContextMenu(Z)
        local b = {}
        for V, V in w do
            local w
            w = j:AddOption(V, function()
                const_0.Mode = V
                w.TextColor3 = const_4.AccentColor
                for u, a in b do
                    if (a ~= w) then
                        a.TextColor3 = u == const_0.Mode and const_4.AccentColor or const_4.FontColor
                    end
                end
                const_4:AttemptSave()
            end)
            w:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextColor3"):Connect(function()
                if (V == const_0.Mode) then
                    w.TextColor3 = const_4.AccentColor
                else
                    w.TextColor3 = const_4.FontColor
                end
            end)
            b[V] = w
        end
        if (const_4.FlagCopying) then
            j:AddOption("Copy Flag", function()
                pcall(setclipboard, const_0.Idx)
                task.wait()
                const_4:Notify("Copied flag to clipboard!", 2)
                j:Hide()
            end)
        end
        for w, j in b do
            j.TextColor3 = w == const_0.Mode and const_4.AccentColor or const_4.FontColor
        end
        local w = function(j)
            local V = i.Mode
            V = V ~= "Always" and const_0.Override and "Override" or V
            const_5.Text = string.format("[%s] %s (%s)", const_0.Value, const_1.Text, V)
            const_5.Visible = true
            const_5.TextColor3 = j and const_4.AccentColor or const_4.FontColor
            const_4.RegistryMap[const_5].Properties.TextColor3 = j and "AccentColor" or "FontColor"
        end
        function i:Update()
            if not const_0.NoUI then
                local M = N.KeypickerListMode
                local j = i:GetState()
                if (M == "Active" and const_0.Parent.Type == "Toggle" and (not j or not const_0.Parent.Value)) then
                    const_5.Visible = false
                elseif (M == "Toggled" and const_0.Parent.Type == "Toggle" and not const_0.Parent.Value) then
                    const_5.Visible = false
                else
                    w(j)
                end
            else
                const_5.Visible = false
            end
            local w = 0
            local M = 0
            for j, j in next, const_4.KeybindContainer:GetChildren() do
                if j:IsA("TextLabel") and j.Visible then
                    w = w + 18
                    if (j.TextBounds.X > M) then
                        M = j.TextBounds.X
                    end
                end
            end
            const_4.KeybindFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(M + 10, 210), 0, w + 23)
            const_4.KeybindFrame.Visible = const_4.KeypickerListVisible and (w ~= 0)
        end
        function i:OverrideState(w)
            if (self.Override == w) then
                return
            end
            self.Override = w
            const_0.Toggled = false
            const_0:Update()
        end
        local w, M = P.IsMouseButtonPressed, P.IsKeyDown
        local j, V = Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1, Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2
        local u = Enum.KeyCode
        function i:GetState()
            local a = i.Mode
            if a == "Always" then
                return true
            end
            local _ = i.Value
            if (_ == "...") then
                return true
            end
            local A = nil
            if (a == "Hold") then
                if (_ == "MB1" or _ == "MB2") then
                    A = _ == "MB1" and w(const_1, j) or _ == "MB2" and w(const_1, V)
                else
                    A = M(const_1, u[_])
                end
            else
                A = const_0.Toggled
            end
            if (A and self.Override) then
                const_0:OverrideState(false)
            end
            return A or self.Override
        end
        function i:SetValue(w)
            local const_1 = constants[1]
            local const_2 = constants[2]
            const_7.Text = M
            const_0.Value, const_0.Mode = M, j
            for w, M in b do
                M.TextColor3 = w == const_0.Mode and const_4.AccentColor or const_4.FontColor
            end
            const_0:Update()
        end
        function i:OnClick(w)
            const_0.Clicked = w
        end
        function i:OnChanged(w)
            const_0.Changed = w
            w(const_0.Value)
        end
        if const_8.Addons then
            table.insert(const_8.Addons, const_0)
        end
        function i:DoClick()
            if (const_0.Override) then
                const_0.Override = false
                const_0.Toggled = false
            end
            if const_8.Type == "Toggle" and const_0.SyncToggleState then
                const_8:SetValue(not const_8.Value)
            end
            const_4:SafeCallback(const_0.Callback, const_0.Toggled)
            const_4:SafeCallback(const_0.Clicked, const_0.Toggled)
        end
        function i:SetupConnection(w)
            table.insert(self.Connections, w)
            const_4:GiveSignal(w)
        end
        function i:Remove()
            const_10[const_6] = nil
            for w, w in const_0.Connections do
                w:Disconnect()
            end
            table.clear(const_0)
            const_2:Destroy()
            const_5:Destroy()
        end
        function i:SetModePickerVisibility(w)
            const_9.Visible = w
        end
        function i:GetModePickerVisibility()
            return const_9.Visible
        end
        local w = false
        local z = { [Enum.KeyCode.Escape] = true, [Enum.KeyCode.Backspace] = true }
        const_2.InputBegan:Connect(function(M)
            if not w and M.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and not const_4:MouseIsOverOpenedFrame() then
                w = true
                const_7.Text = ""
                local M
                local Z = ""
                task.spawn(function()
                    while (not M) do
                        if Z == "..." then
                            Z = ""
                        end
                        Z = Z .. "."
                        const_7.Text = Z
                        wait(0.4)
                    end
                end)
                wait(0.2)
                local Z
                Z = const_1.InputBegan:Connect(function(o)
                    local Q
                    if o.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                        Q = z[o.KeyCode] and "..." or o.KeyCode.Name
                    elseif o.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                        Q = "MB1"
                    elseif o.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
                        Q = "MB2"
                    end
                    M = true
                    w = false
                    const_7.Text = Q
                    const_0.Value = Q
                    const_4:SafeCallback(const_0.ChangedCallback, o.KeyCode or o.UserInputType)
                    const_4:SafeCallback(const_0.Changed, o.KeyCode or o.UserInputType)
                    const_4:AttemptSave()
                    Z:Disconnect()
                end)
            end
        end)
        const_0:SetupConnection(const_1.InputBegan:Connect(function(S, z)
            if (not w and not z) then
                if const_0.Mode == "Toggle" then
                    local z = i.Value
                    if z == "MB1" or z == "MB2" then
                        if z == "MB1" and S.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or z == "MB2" and S.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
                            const_0.Toggled = not const_0.Toggled
                            const_0:DoClick()
                        end
                    elseif S.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                        if S.KeyCode.Name == z then
                            const_0.Toggled = not const_0.Toggled
                            const_0:DoClick()
                        end
                    end
                end
                const_0:Update()
            end
        end))
        const_0:SetupConnection(const_1.InputEnded:Connect(function(S)
            if (not w) then
                const_0:Update()
            end
        end))
        const_0:Update()
        const_10[const_6] = const_0
    end
end

local ui_tabbox_module = function(constants)
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_8 = constants[8]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_7 = constants[7]
    local const_6 = constants[6]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_9 = constants[9]
    return function()
        local w = M.Tabboxes
        function z:Remove()
            const_4:Destroy()
            table.clear(const_3)
            w[const_6.Name or ""] = nil
        end
        function z:AddTab(w, P)
            local j = {}
            local b = T:Create(Z[3][Z[2]], { BackgroundColor3 = T.MainColor, BorderSizePixel = 1, BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0), ZIndex = 6, Parent = Q })

                                elseif z == "Uri" then
                                    self:_newImage(Z, true)
                                elseif z == "Visible" then
                                    self._frame.Visible = Z
                                elseif z == "ZIndex" then
                                    self._frame.ZIndex = Z
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    function w:__iter()
                        return next, self._properties
                    end
                    function w:__tostring()
                        return "Drawing"
                    end
                    function w:Destroy()
                        _[self._id] = nil
                        self.__OBJECT_EXISTS = false
                        P(self._frame)
                    end
                    function w:_newImage(S, z)
                        k(function()
                            self._imageId = self._imageId + 1
                            local M = C("%s-%s.png", self._id, self._imageId)
                            if z then
                                local z
                                while z == nil do
                                    local Z, o = pcall(f, game, S, true)
                                    if Z then
                                        z = o
                                    elseif string.find(string.lower(o), "too many requests") then
                                        task.wait(3)
                                    else
                                        error(o, 2)
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                                self._properties.Data = S
                            else
                                self._properties.Uri = ""
                            end
                            self._frame.Image = x(M, S)
                        end)
                    end
                    w.Remove = w.Destroy
                    X.Image = w
                end
                l.new = newcclosure(function(w)
                    return V(X[w], C("Invalid drawing type '%s'", w)).new()
                end)
                l.clear = newcclosure(function()
                    for w, w in _ do
                        if w.__OBJECT_EXISTS then
                            w:Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                end)
                l.cache = _
            end
            setreadonly(l, true)
            setreadonly(l.Fonts, true)
            return l
        end
    end
end

local ui_container_resize_helper = function(constants)
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_6 = constants[6]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    return function()
        function T:Resize()
            const_3.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, const_6.AbsoluteContentSize.Y)
            const_4:Resize()
        end
        const_6:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
            const_1:Resize()
        end)
        const_3:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
            const_1:Resize()
        end)
        function T:Update()
            local w = false
            for o, o in next, const_1.Dependencies do
                if (o[1].Value == o[2]) then
                    w = true
                    break
                end
            end
            const_3.Visible = w
            const_1:Resize()
        end
        function T:SetupDependencies(w)
            for o, o in next, w do
                assert(type(o) == "table", "SetupDependencies: Dependency is not of type `table`.")
                assert(o[1], "SetupDependencies: Dependency is missing element argument.")
                assert(o[2] ~= nil, "SetupDependencies: Dependency is missing value argument.")
            end
            const_1.Dependencies = w
            const_1:Update()
        end
        function T:Remove()
            table.remove(const_0.DependencyBoxes, table.find(const_0.DependencyBoxes, const_1))
            table.clear(const_1)
            const_3:Destroy()
            const_4:Resize()
        end
        const_1.Container = const_5
        setmetatable(const_1, const_2)
        table.insert(const_0.DependencyBoxes, const_1)
    end
end

local ui_dropdown_module = function(constants)
    local const_7 = constants[7]
    local const_6 = constants[6]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_9 = constants[9]
    local const_13 = constants[13]
    local const_14 = constants[14]
    local const_10 = constants[10]
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_11 = constants[11]
    local const_15 = constants[15]
    local const_12 = constants[12]
    local const_8 = constants[8]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    return function()
        local function w()
            const_2.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(const_8.AbsolutePosition.X, const_8.AbsolutePosition.Y + const_8.Size.Y.Offset + 1)
        end
        local function _(A)
            local H = A or math.clamp(#M.Values * 20, 0, S * 20) + 1
            const_2.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(const_8.AbsoluteSize.X + 0.5, H)
        end
        w()
        _()
        const_8:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsolutePosition"):Connect(w)
        local w = a:Create(P[3][P[2]], { BackgroundColor3 = a.MainColor, BorderColor3 = a.OutlineColor, BorderMode = Enum.BorderMode.Inset, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), ZIndex = 21, Parent = Z })
        const_1:AddToRegistry(w, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor", BorderColor3 = const_4[3][const_4[2]] })
        local P = a:Create("ScrollingFrame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), ZIndex = 21, Parent = w, TopImage = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Scroll/scroll-middle.png", BottomImage = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Scroll/scroll-middle.png", ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, ScrollBarThickness = a.IsMobile and 15 or 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = a.AccentColor })
        const_1:AddToRegistry(P, { ScrollBarImageColor3 = "AccentColor" })
        const_1:Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 0), FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = P })
        function M:Display()
            local w = M.Values
            local A = ""
            if const_3.Multi then
                for H, H in next, w do
                    if const_0.Value[H] then
                        A = A .. H .. ", "
                    end
                end
                A = A:sub(1, #A - 2)
            else
                A = const_0.Value or ""
            end
            const_11.Text = (A == "" and "..." or A)
        end
        function M:GetActiveValues()
            if const_3.Multi then
                local w = {}
                for l, A in next, const_0.Value do
                    table.insert(w, l)
                end
                return w
            else
                return const_0.Value and 1 or 0
            end
        end
        function M:BuildDropdownList()
            local w = M.Values
            local l = {}
            for A, A in next, P:GetChildren() do
                if not A:IsA("UIListLayout") then
                    A:Destroy()
                end
            end
            local A = 0
            for H, H in next, w do
                local w = {}
                A = A + 1
                local g = a:Create("ImageButton", { BackgroundColor3 = a.MainColor, BorderColor3 = a.OutlineColor, BorderMode = Enum.BorderMode.Middle, Size = UDim2.new(1, -1, 0, 20), ZIndex = 23, Active = true, Parent = P, Image = "", Active = false, AutoButtonColor = false })
                const_1:AddToRegistry(g, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor", BorderColor3 = const_4[3][const_4[2]] })
                local C = a:CreateLabel({ Active = false, Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 0), TextSize = 14, Text = H, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 25, Parent = g })
                const_1:OnHighlight(g, g, { BorderColor3 = const_4[3][const_4[2]], ZIndex = 24 }, { BorderColor3 = const_4[3][const_4[2]], ZIndex = 23 })
                local N
                if const_3.Multi then
                    N = const_0.Value[H]
                else
                    N = const_0.Value == H
                end
                function w:UpdateButton()
                    if const_3.Multi then
                        N = const_0.Value[H]
                    else
                        N = const_0.Value == H
                    end
                    C.TextColor3 = N and const_1.AccentColor or const_1.FontColor
                    const_1.RegistryMap[C].Properties.TextColor3 = N and "AccentColor" or "FontColor"
                end
                C.InputBegan:Connect(function(C)
                    if C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or C.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                        local C = not N
                        if const_0:GetActiveValues() == 1 and (not C) and (not const_3.AllowNull) then
                        else
                            if const_3.Multi then
                                N = C
                                if N then
                                    const_0.Value[H] = true
                                else
                                    const_0.Value[H] = nil
                                end
                            else
                                N = C
                                if N then
                                    const_0.Value = H
                                else
                                    const_0.Value = nil
                                end
                                for N, N in next, l do
                                    N:UpdateButton()
                                end
                            end
                            w:UpdateButton()
                            const_0:Display()
                            const_1:UpdateDependencyBoxes()
                            const_1:SafeCallback(const_0.Callback, const_0.Value)
                            const_1:SafeCallback(const_0.Changed, const_0.Value)
                            const_1:AttemptSave()
                        end
                    end
                end)
                w:UpdateButton()
                l[g] = w
            end
            const_0:Display()
            P.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, (A * 20) + 1)
            P.Visible = false
            P.Visible = true
            local w = math.clamp(A * 20, 0, S * 20) + 1
            _(w)
        end
        function M:SetValues(w)
            if w then
                const_0.Values = w
            end
            const_0:BuildDropdownList()
        end
        function M:OpenDropdown()
            if const_1.IsMobile then
                const_1.CanDrag = false
            end
            const_2.Visible = true
            const_1.OpenedFrames[const_2] = true
            const_10.Rotation = 90
            _()
        end
        function M:CloseDropdown()
            if const_1.IsMobile then
                const_1.CanDrag = true
            end
            const_2.Visible = false
            const_1.OpenedFrames[const_2] = nil
            const_10.Rotation = 0
        end
        function M:OnChanged(w)
            const_0.Changed = w
            w(const_0.Value)
        end
        function M:SetValue(w)
            if const_0.Multi then
                local S = {}
                for Q, N in next, w do
                    if const_0.Illegal or table.find(const_0.Values, Q) then
                        S[Q] = true
                    end
                end
                const_0.Value = S
            else
                if (not w) then
                    const_0.Value = nil
                elseif const_0.Illegal or table.find(const_0.Values, w) then
                    const_0.Value = w
                end
            end
            const_0:BuildDropdownList()
            const_1:SafeCallback(const_0.Callback, const_0.Value)
            const_1:SafeCallback(const_0.Changed, const_0.Value)
        end
        function M:Remove()
            for w, w in const_13 do
                w:Destroy()
            end
            const_6[const_12] = nil
            const_8:Destroy()
            table.clear(const_0)
        end
        const_8.InputBegan:Connect(function(w)
            if (const_1.IsDragging) then
                return
            end
            if (w.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and not const_1:MouseIsOverOpenedFrame()) or w.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                if const_2.Visible then
                    const_0:CloseDropdown()
                else
                    const_0:OpenDropdown()
                end
            end
        end)
        const_14.InputBegan:Connect(function(w)
            if (const_1.IsDragging) then
                return
            end
            if w.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or w.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                local w, S = Z.AbsolutePosition, Z.AbsoluteSize
                if const_9.X < w.X or const_9.X > w.X + S.X or const_9.Y < (w.Y - 20 - 1) or const_9.Y > w.Y + S.Y then
                    const_0:CloseDropdown()
                end
            end
        end)
        if (const_1.FlagCopying) then
            local w = a:AddContextMenu(b)
            w:AddOption("Copy Flag", function()
                pcall(setclipboard, const_0.Idx)
                task.wait()
                const_1:Notify("Copied flag to clipboard!", 2)
                w:Hide()
            end)
        end
        const_0:BuildDropdownList()
        const_0:Display()
        local w = {}
        if typeof(const_3.Default) == "string" then
            local S = table.find(M.Values, V.Default)
            if S then
                table.insert(w, S)
            end
        elseif typeof(const_3.Default) == "table" then
            for S, S in next, const_3.Default do
                local Z = table.find(M.Values, S)
                if Z then
                    table.insert(w, Z)
                end
            end
        elseif typeof(const_3.Default) == "number" and const_0.Values[const_3.Default] ~= nil then
            table.insert(w, const_3.Default)
        end
        if next(w) then
            for S = 1, #w do
                local Z = w[S]
                if const_3.Multi then
                    const_0.Value[const_0.Values[Z]] = true
                else
                    const_0.Value = const_0.Values[Z]
                end
                if (not const_3.Multi) then
                    break
                end
            end
            const_0:BuildDropdownList()
            const_0:Display()
        end
        table.insert(const_13, const_5:AddBlank(const_3.BlankSize or 5))
        const_5:Resize()
        const_6[const_12] = const_0
    end
end

local ui_tab_module = function(constants)
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_9 = constants[9]
    local const_7 = constants[7]
    local const_8 = constants[8]
    local const_11 = constants[11]
    local const_6 = constants[6]
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_10 = constants[10]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    return function()
        local w = {}
        local w = false
        local b = false
        function j:Toggle()
            if b then
                return
            end
            w = (not w)
            const_2.Toggled = w
            const_4[3][const_4[2]] = w
            const_2:FireEvent("VisibilityChanged", const_4[3][const_4[2]])
            const_5.Modal = w
            if w then
                const_9.Visible = true
                local S = game:GetService("GuiService")
                task.spawn(function()
                    local l = N.MouseIconEnabled
                    local b = Instance.new("ImageLabel", i)
                    b.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=4292970642"
                    b.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    b.ZIndex = 125
                    local V = Instance.new("ImageLabel", i)
                    V.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=4292970642"
                    V.ImageColor3 = Color3.new()
                    V.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    V.ZIndex = 124
                    b.Size, V.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(17, 17), UDim2.fromOffset(19, 19)
                    b.Rotation, V.Rotation = -45, -45
                    while w and const_7.Parent do
                        const_6.MouseIconEnabled = false
                        local i = N:GetMouseLocation()
                        local u = UDim2.fromOffset(i.X, i.Y - S:GetGuiInset().Y - 1)
                        b.ImageColor3 = const_2.AccentColor
                        b.Position, V.Position = u, u - UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1)
                        const_1:Wait()
                    end
                    const_6.MouseIconEnabled = l
                    b:Destroy()
                    V:Destroy()
                end)
            end
            for S, S in const_11 do
                task.spawn(function()
                    if S.Type == "Dropdown" then
                        S:CloseDropdown()
                    elseif S.Type == "KeyPicker" then
                        S:SetModePickerVisibility(false)
                    elseif S.Type == "ColorPicker" then
                        S:Hide()
                    end
                end)
            end
            if w and const_2.BlockInput then
                const_0:BindAction("Freeze", function()
                    return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
                end, false, table.unpack(Enum.PlayerActions:GetEnumItems()))
            else
                const_0:UnbindAction("Freeze")
            end
            const_3.Enabled = w
            const_10.Enabled = w
            const_8.Enabled = w
            const_9.Visible = w
        end
        const_2:GiveSignal(const_6.InputBegan:Connect(function(w, S)
            if typeof(const_2.ToggleKeybind) == "table" and const_2.ToggleKeybind.Type == "KeyPicker" then
                if w.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and w.KeyCode.Name == const_2.ToggleKeybind.Value then
                    task.spawn(const_2.Toggle)
                end
            elseif w.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightControl or (w.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Insert and (not S)) then
                task.spawn(const_2.Toggle)
            end
        end))
    end
end

local ui_colorpicker_module = function(constants)
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_6 = constants[6]
    local const_10 = constants[10]
    local const_23 = constants[23]
    local const_7 = constants[7]
    local const_20 = constants[20]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_21 = constants[21]
    local const_8 = constants[8]
    local const_22 = constants[22]
    local const_18 = constants[18]
    local const_13 = constants[13]
    local const_17 = constants[17]
    local const_12 = constants[12]
    local const_11 = constants[11]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_9 = constants[9]
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_15 = constants[15]
    local const_16 = constants[16]
    local const_14 = constants[14]
    local const_19 = constants[19]
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_24 = constants[24]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    return function()
        local w = r:AddContextMenu(H)
        w:AddOption("Make gradient", function()
            local D = {}
            for n, n in const_18.Addons do
                if (n.Type == "ColorPicker") then
                    table.insert(D, n)
                end
            end
            if (#D < 3) then
                w:Hide()
                return const_3:Notify("not enough colors for a gradient.", 2)
            end
            local n, f = D[1].Value, D[#D].Value
            for x = 2, #D - 1 do
                local W = D[x]
                W:SetValueRGB(n:Lerp(f, x / #D), W.Transparency)
            end
            const_3:Notify("created gradient!", 2)
            w:Hide()
        end)
        w:AddOption("Match color", function()
            local D = {}
            for n, n in const_18.Addons do
                if (n.Type == "ColorPicker") then
                    table.insert(D, n)
                end
            end
            for P, P in D do
                P:SetValueRGB(const_0.Value, P.Transparency)
            end
            const_3:Notify("matched all colors!", 2)
            w:Hide()
        end)
        w:AddOption("Copy color", function()
            const_3.ColorClipboard = const_0
            const_3:Notify("Copied color!", 2)
            w:Hide()
        end)
        w:AddOption("Paste color", function()
            if not const_3.ColorClipboard then
                return const_3:Notify("You have not copied a color!", 2)
            end
            const_0:SetValueRGB(const_3.ColorClipboard.Value, const_3.ColorClipboard.Transparency)
            w:Hide()
        end)
        if (const_3.FlagCopying) then
            w:AddOption("Copy Flag", function()
                pcall(setclipboard, const_0.Idx)
                task.wait()
                const_3:Notify("Copied flag to clipboard!", 2)
                w:Hide()
            end)
        end
        const_3:AddToRegistry(const_13, { BackgroundColor3 = "BackgroundColor", BorderColor3 = const_2[3][const_2[2]] })
        const_3:AddToRegistry(const_16, { BackgroundColor3 = "AccentColor" })
        const_3:AddToRegistry(const_21, { BackgroundColor3 = "BackgroundColor", BorderColor3 = const_2[3][const_2[2]] })
        const_3:AddToRegistry(const_20, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor", BorderColor3 = const_2[3][const_2[2]] })
        const_3:AddToRegistry(const_24.Frame, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor", BorderColor3 = const_2[3][const_2[2]] })
        const_3:AddToRegistry(const_6, { TextColor3 = "FontColor" })
        const_3:AddToRegistry(const_7, { TextColor3 = "FontColor" })
        local w = {}
        for i = 0, 1, 0.1 do
            table.insert(w, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(i, Color3.fromHSV(i, 1, 1)))
        end
        local i = r:Create("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(w), Rotation = 90, Parent = u })
        const_7.FocusLost:Connect(function(w)
            if w then
                local w, i = pcall(Color3.fromHex, o.Text)
                if w and typeof(i) == "Color3" then
                    const_0.Hue, const_0.Sat, const_0.Vib = Color3.toHSV(i)
                end
            end
            const_0:Display()
        end)
        const_6.FocusLost:Connect(function(w)
            if w then
                local w, i, Q = z.Text:match("(%d+),%s*(%d+),%s*(%d+)")
                if w and i and Q then
                    const_0.Hue, const_0.Sat, const_0.Vib = Color3.toHSV(Color3.fromRGB(w, i, Q))
                end
            end
            const_0:Display()
        end)
        function S:Display()
            const_0.Value = Color3.fromHSV(const_0.Hue, const_0.Sat, const_0.Vib)
            const_12.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(const_0.Hue, 1, 1)
            const_3:Create(const_15, { BackgroundColor3 = const_0.Value, BackgroundTransparency = const_0.Transparency, BorderColor3 = const_3:GetDarkerColor(const_0.Value) })
            if const_14[3][const_14[2]] then
                const_14[3][const_14[2]].BackgroundColor3 = const_0.Value
                const_23[3][const_23[2]].Position = UDim2.new(1 - const_0.Transparency, 0, 0, 0)
            end
            const_9.Position = UDim2.new(const_0.Sat, 0, 1 - const_0.Vib, 0)
            const_22.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, const_0.Hue, 0)
            const_7.Text = "#" .. const_0.Value:ToHex()
            const_6.Text = table.concat({ math.floor(const_0.Value.R * 255), math.floor(const_0.Value.G * 255), math.floor(const_0.Value.B * 255) }, ", ")
            const_3:SafeCallback(const_0.Callback, const_0.Value)
            const_3:SafeCallback(const_0.Changed, const_0.Value)
        end
        function S:OnChanged(w)
            const_0.Changed = w
            w(const_0.Value)
        end
        local w = false
        function S:Show()
            w = true
            for z, Z in next, const_3.OpenedFrames do
                if z.Name == "Color" then
                    z.Visible = false
                    const_3.OpenedFrames[z] = nil
                end
            end
            const_10.Visible = true
            const_3.OpenedFrames[const_10] = true
        end
        function S:Hide()
            w = false
            const_10.Visible = false
            const_3.OpenedFrames[const_10] = nil
        end
        function S:SetValue(z, Z)
            local o = Color3.fromHSV(z[1], z[2], z[3])
            const_0.Transparency = Z or 0
            const_0:SetHSVFromRGB(o)
            const_0:Display()
        end
        function S:Remove()
            const_8[const_19] = nil
            table.clear(const_0)
        end
        function S:SetValueRGB(z, Z)
            const_0.Transparency = Z or 0
            const_0:SetHSVFromRGB(z)
            const_0:Display()
        end
        const_12.InputBegan:Connect(function(z)
            if (const_3.IsDragging) then
                return
            end
            if (z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local Z = true
                local o
                if (z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    o = z.Changed:Connect(function()
                        if (z.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End) then
                            Z = false
                        end
                    end)
                end
                while (Z and ((z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and const_17:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)) or z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch)) do
                    local Z = (z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) and v.X or z.Position.X
                    local i = (z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) and v.Y or z.Position.Y
                    local z, Q = V.AbsolutePosition.X, V.AbsolutePosition.X + V.AbsoluteSize.X
                    local l, P = V.AbsolutePosition.Y, V.AbsolutePosition.Y + V.AbsoluteSize.Y
                    local j = math.clamp(Z, z, Q)
                    local Z = math.clamp(i, l, P)
                    const_0.Sat = (j - z) / (Q - z)
                    const_0.Vib = 1 - ((Z - l) / (P - l))
                    const_0:Display()
                    const_1:Wait()
                end
                if (o) then
                    o:Disconnect()
                end
                const_3:AttemptSave()
            end
        end)
        const_11.InputBegan:Connect(function(z)
            if (const_3.IsDragging) then
                return
            end
            if (z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local Z = true
                local o
                if (z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    o = z.Changed:Connect(function()
                        if (z.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End) then
                            Z = false
                        end
                    end)
                end
                while (Z and ((z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and const_17:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)) or z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch)) do
                    local Z = (z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) and v.Y or z.Position.Y
                    local z, i = u.AbsolutePosition.Y, u.AbsolutePosition.Y + u.AbsoluteSize.Y
                    local Q = math.clamp(Z, z, i)
                    const_0.Hue = ((Q - z) / (i - z))
                    const_0:Display()
                    const_1:Wait()
                end
                if (o) then
                    o:Disconnect()
                end
                const_3:AttemptSave()
            end
        end)
        const_15.InputBegan:Connect(function(z)
            if (const_3.IsDragging) then
                return
            end
            if (z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                if (not const_3:MouseIsOverOpenedFrame()) then
                    if (const_10.Visible) then
                        const_0:Hide()
                    else
                        const_0:Show()
                    end
                end
            elseif (z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 and not const_3:MouseIsOverOpenedFrame()) then
                const_0:Hide()
            end
        end)
        if const_14[3][const_14[2]] then
            const_14[3][const_14[2]].InputBegan:Connect(function(z)
                if (const_3.IsDragging) then
                    return
                end
                if (z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    local Z = true
                    local o
                    if (z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                        o = z.Changed:Connect(function()
                            if (z.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End) then
                                Z = false
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    while (Z and ((z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and const_17:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)) or z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch)) do
                        local Z = (z.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) and v.X or z.Position.X
                        local z, i = C[3][C[2]].AbsolutePosition.X, C[3][C[2]].AbsolutePosition.X + C[3][C[2]].AbsoluteSize.X
                        local Q = math.clamp(Z, z, i)
                        const_0.Transparency = 1 - ((Q - z) / (i - z))
                        const_0:Display()
                        const_1:Wait()
                    end
                    if (o) then
                        o:Disconnect()
                    end
                    const_3:AttemptSave()
                end
            end)
        end
        local z = function()
            if (not w) then
                return
            end
            local w, Z = M.AbsolutePosition, M.AbsoluteSize
            if (const_5.X < w.X or const_5.X > w.X + Z.X or const_5.Y < (w.Y - 20 - 1) or const_5.Y > w.Y + Z.Y) then
                const_0:Hide()
            end
        end
        for w, w in { Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1, Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 } do
            const_3:BindToInput(w, const_6)
        end
        const_0:Display()
        const_0.DisplayFrame = const_15
        const_8[const_19] = const_0
        const_4.Addons = const_4.Addons or {}
        table.insert(const_4.Addons, const_0)
        if (const_4.ToggleRegion) then
            const_4.ColorPickerCount = const_4.ColorPickerCount + 1
            if (const_4.ColorPickerCount > 2) then
                const_4.ToggleRegion.Size = const_4.ToggleRegion.Size - UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 0)
            end
        end
    end
end

local ui_tab_manager_module = function(constants)
    local const_15 = constants[15]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_10 = constants[10]
    local const_12 = constants[12]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_13 = constants[13]
    local const_7 = constants[7]
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_11 = constants[11]
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_6 = constants[6]
    local const_8 = constants[8]
    local const_14 = constants[14]
    local const_9 = constants[9]
    return function()
        if const_1.IsMobile then
            local w = { Left = tick(), Right = tick() }
            const_3:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(function()
                const_1.CanDrag = false
                local _ = tick()
                w.Left = _
                task.wait(0.15)
                if w.Left == _ then
                    const_1.CanDrag = true
                end
            end)
            const_5:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(function()
                const_1.CanDrag = false
                local _ = tick()
                w.Right = _
                task.wait(0.15)
                if w.Right == _ then
                    const_1.CanDrag = true
                end
            end)
        end
        for w, w in next, { const_3, const_5 } do
            w:WaitForChild("UIListLayout"):GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
                w.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, w.UIListLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y)
            end)
        end
        function i:Resize()
            if const_4[3][const_4[2]].Visible == true then
                local w = 10
                for _, _ in next, const_14[3][const_14[2]]:GetChildren() do
                    if (not _:IsA("UIListLayout")) and _.Visible then
                        w = w + _.Size.Y.Offset
                    end
                end
                const_4[3][const_4[2]].Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, w)
                w = w + 10
                const_3.Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 7 + w)
                const_3.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -10, 1, -14 - w)
                const_5.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 5, 0, 7 + w)
                const_5.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -10, 1, -14 - w)
            else
                const_3.Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 7)
                const_3.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -10, 1, -14)
                const_5.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 5, 0, 7)
                const_5.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -10, 1, -14)
            end
        end
        function i:UpdateWarningBox(w)
            if typeof(w.Visible) == "boolean" then
                const_4[3][const_4[2]].Visible = w.Visible
                const_2:Resize()
            end
            if typeof(w.Title) == "string" then
                const_11[3][const_11[2]].Text = w.Title
            end
            if typeof(w.Text) == "string" then
                const_9[3][const_9[2]].Text = w.Text
                local l = select(2, j:GetTextBounds(w.Text, j.Font, 14, Vector2.new(a[3][a[2]].AbsoluteSize.X, math.huge)))
                const_9[3][const_9[2]].Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, l)
                const_2:Resize()
            end
        end
        function i:ShowTab()
            const_1.ActiveTab = const_10
            for w, w in next, const_12.Tabs do
                w:HideTab()
            end
            const_1.RegistryMap[const_6].Properties.BackgroundColor3 = "BackgroundColor"
            const_6.BackgroundColor3 = const_1.MainColor
            const_13.Visible = true
            const_15.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            const_7.TextColor3 = const_1.FontColor
            const_8.Visible = true
            const_2:Resize()
        end
        function i:HideTab()
            const_6.BackgroundColor3 = const_1.BackgroundColor
            const_1.RegistryMap[const_6].Properties.BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor"
            const_15.Color = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
            const_7.TextColor3 = const_1:GetDarkerColor(const_1.FontColor)
            const_8.Visible = false
            const_13.Visible = false
        end
        function i:SetLayoutOrder(w)
            const_6.LayoutOrder = w
            const_0:ApplyLayout()
        end
        function i:GetSides()
            return { Left = const_3, Right = const_5 }
        end
        function i:AddLeftGroupbox(w)
            return self:AddGroupbox({ Side = 1, Name = w })
        end
        function i:AddRightGroupbox(w)
            return self:AddGroupbox({ Side = 2, Name = w })
        end
        function i:AddLeftTabbox(w)
            return const_2:AddTabbox({ Name = w, Side = 1 })
        end
        function i:AddRightTabbox(w)
            return const_2:AddTabbox({ Name = w, Side = 2 })
        end
        function i:Remove()
            table.clear(const_2)
            const_13:Destroy()
            const_6:Destroy()
            const_12.Tabs[const_10] = nil
        end
        const_6.InputBegan:Connect(function(w)
            if (const_1.IsDragging) then
                return
            end
            if w.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or w.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                const_2:ShowTab()
            end
        end)
        const_4[3][const_4[2]]:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
            const_2:Resize()
        end)
        const_1.TotalTabs = const_1.TotalTabs + 1
        if const_1.TotalTabs == 1 then
            const_2:ShowTab()
        end
        const_12.Tabs[const_10] = const_2
    end
end

local ui_textinput_module = function(constants)
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_10 = constants[10]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_7 = constants[7]
    local const_9 = constants[9]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_8 = constants[8]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_6 = constants[6]
    return function()
        function M:SetValue(w)
            if const_2.MaxLength and #w > const_2.MaxLength then
                w = w:sub(1, const_2.MaxLength)
            end
            if const_0.Numeric then
                if (not tonumber(w)) and w:len() > 0 then
                    w = const_0.Value
                end
            end
            const_0.Value = w
            const_1.Text = w
            const_4:SafeCallback(const_0.Callback, const_0.Value)
            const_4:SafeCallback(const_0.Changed, const_0.Value)
        end
        function M:Remove()
            for w, w in const_7 do
                w:Destroy()
            end
            const_3[const_5] = nil
            const_9:Destroy()
            table.clear(const_0)
            const_6:Resize()
        end
        if const_0.Finished then
            const_1.FocusLost:Connect(function(w)
                const_0:SetValue(const_1.Text)
                const_4:AttemptSave()
            end)
        else
            const_1:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
                const_0:SetValue(const_1.Text)
                const_4:AttemptSave()
            end)
        end
        local function w()
            local l = 2
            local j = z.AbsoluteSize.X
            if not const_1:IsFocused() or const_1.TextBounds.X <= j - 2 * l then
                const_1.Position = UDim2.new(0, l, 0, 0)
            else
                local z = T.CursorPosition
                if z ~= -1 then
                    local b = string.sub(T.Text, 1, z - 1)
                    local z = N:GetTextSize(b, T.TextSize, T.Font, Vector2.new(math.huge, math.huge)).X
                    local N = T.Position.X.Offset + z
                    if N < l then
                        const_1.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(l - z, 0)
                    elseif N > j - l - 1 then
                        const_1.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(j - z - l - 1, 0)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        task.spawn(w)
        const_1:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(w)
        const_1:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CursorPosition"):Connect(w)
        const_1.FocusLost:Connect(w)
        const_1.Focused:Connect(w)
        if (const_4.FlagCopying) then
            local w = Q:AddContextMenu(i)
            w:AddOption("Copy Flag", function()
                pcall(setclipboard, const_0.Idx)
                task.wait()
                const_4:Notify("Copied flag to clipboard!", 2)
                w:Hide()
            end)
        end
        const_4:AddToRegistry(const_1, { TextColor3 = "FontColor" })
        function M:OnChanged(w)
            const_0.Changed = w
            w(const_0.Value)
        end
        table.insert(const_7, const_6:AddBlank(5))
        const_6:Resize()
        const_3[const_5] = const_0
    end
end

local ui_button_module = function(constants)
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    return function()
        function z:AddButton(...)
            local w = typeof(select(1, ...)) == "table" and select(1, ...) or { Text = select(1, ...), Func = select(2, ...) }
            assert(typeof(w.Func) == "function", "AddButton: `Func` callback is missing.")
            self.Outer.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -2, 0, 20)
            w.Outer, w.Inner, w.Label = const_3(w)
            w.Outer.Position = UDim2.new(1, 3, 0, 0)
            w.Outer.Size = UDim2.new(1, -3, 1, 0)
            w.Outer.Parent = self.Outer
            function w:AddTooltip(S)
                if typeof(S) == "string" then
                    const_4:AddToolTip(S, self.Outer)
                end
                return w
            end
            if typeof(w.Tooltip) == "string" then
                w:AddTooltip(w.Tooltip)
            end
            const_0(w)
            return w
        end
        local w = {}
        function z:Remove()
            for S, S in w do
                S:Destroy()
            end
            const_1.Outer:Destroy()
            table.clear(const_1)
            const_2:Resize()
        end
        if typeof(const_1.Tooltip) == "string" then
            const_1:AddTooltip(const_1.Tooltip)
        end
        table.insert(w, const_2:AddBlank(5))
        const_2:Resize()
    end
end

local ui_line_module = function(constants)
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_10 = constants[10]
    local const_13 = constants[13]
    local const_7 = constants[7]
    local const_9 = constants[9]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_16 = constants[16]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_6 = constants[6]
    local const_15 = constants[15]
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_17 = constants[17]
    local const_12 = constants[12]
    local const_11 = constants[11]
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_8 = constants[8]
    local const_14 = constants[14]
    return function(w, H, g)
        local C, k = _("Line"), _("Line")
        local _ = A(1, i(M.Value))
        C.Thickness, k.Thickness = _ + 2, _
        C.Color = Color3.new()
        local M = Instance.new("UIGradient", k._frame)
        M.Color = g
        M.Rotation = 0
        local i = Instance.new("UIGradient", C._frame)
        i.Color = const_12
        i.Rotation = 0
        i.Transparency, M.Transparency = const_7[3][const_7[2]], const_7[3][const_7[2]]
        k.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        k.ZIndex = 2
        local Z, V = nil, 0
        local _, A = a.Value, u.Value
        local u = P.Value
        local P = l.Value
        local l = H
        local a = b.CFrame.Position
        Z = const_1.RenderStepped:Connect(function(Q)
            V = V + Q
            if (V > (_ + A)) then
                C:Remove()
                k:Remove()
                return Z:Disconnect()
            end
            if (u ~= 0) then
                local Z = o(z(V / u, 1))
                l = w:Lerp(H, Z)
            end
            local z, Z = j[3][j[2]](w)
            if (not Z) then
                C.Visible, k.Visible = false, false
                return
            end
            local w, Z = j[3][j[2]](l)
            if (not Z) then
                C.Visible, k.Visible = false, false
                return
            end
            if (V > _) then
                local Z = (V - _) / A
                local o = {}
                for Q = 0, 9 do
                    local l = Q / 9
                    const_2(o, const_0(l, Z + ((1 - l) / 2)))
                end
                o = const_16(o)
                M.Transparency = o
                if (P) then
                    i.Transparency = o
                end
            end
            if (P) then
                local S = (w - z).Unit
                C.To, C.From = w + S, z - S
                C.Visible = true
            else
                C.Visible = false
            end
            k.To, k.From = w, z
            k.Visible = true
        end)
    end
end

    end
