local save_manager_module = function(constants)
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    return function()
        local w = cloneref or function(o)
            return o
        end
        local o = w(game:GetService("HttpService"))
        local w, i, T = isfolder, isfile, listfiles
        do
            const_0.Folder = ""
            const_0.Ignore = {}
            const_0.Parser = { Toggle = { Save = function(Q, N)
                return { type = "Toggle", idx = Q, value = N.Value }
            end, Load = function(Q, N)
                if const_2[Q] then
                    const_2[Q]:SetValue(N.value)
                end
            end }, Slider = { Save = function(Q, N)
                return { type = "Slider", idx = Q, value = tostring(N.Value) }
            end, Load = function(Q, N)
                if const_1[Q] then
                    const_1[Q]:SetValue(N.value)
                end
            end }, Dropdown = { Save = function(Q, N)
                return { type = "Dropdown", idx = Q, value = N.Value, mutli = N.Multi }
            end, Load = function(Q, N)
                if const_1[Q] then
                    const_1[Q]:SetValue(N.value)
                end
            end }, ColorPicker = { Save = function(Q, N)
                return { type = "ColorPicker", idx = Q, value = N.Value:ToHex(), transparency = N.Transparency }
            end, Load = function(Q, N)
                if const_1[Q] then
                    const_1[Q]:SetValueRGB(Color3.fromHex(N.value), N.transparency)
                end
            end }, KeyPicker = { Save = function(Q, N)
                return { type = "KeyPicker", idx = Q, mode = N.Mode, key = N.Value }
            end, Load = function(Q, N)
                if const_1[Q] then
                    const_1[Q]:SetValue({ N.key, N.mode })
                end
            end }, Input = { Save = function(Q, N)
                return { type = "Input", idx = Q, text = N.Value }
            end, Load = function(Q, N)
                if const_1[Q] and type(N.text) == "string" then
                    const_1[Q]:SetValue(N.text)
                end
            end } }
            function Z:BuildFolderTree()
                local Q = { self.Folder, self.Folder .. "/settings", self.Folder .. "/themes" }
                for N = 1, #Q do
                    local l = Q[N]
                    if not w(l) then
                        makefolder(l)
                    end
                end
            end
            function Z:CheckFolderTree()
                if not w(self.Folder) then
                    const_0:BuildFolderTree()
                    task.wait()
                end
            end
            function Z:SetIgnoreIndexes(w)
                for Q, Q in next, w do
                    self.Ignore[Q] = true
                end
            end
            function Z:SetFolder(w)
                self.Folder = w
                self:BuildFolderTree()
            end
            function Z:Save(w, Q)
                if (not w) then
                    return false, "no config file is selected"
                end
                const_0:CheckFolderTree()
                local N = self.Folder .. "/settings/" .. w .. ".json"
                local w = { objects = {} }
                for l, P in next, const_2 do
                    if self.Ignore[l] then
                        continue
                    end
                    table.insert(w.objects, self.Parser[P.Type].Save(l, P))
                end
                for S, l in next, const_1 do
                    if not self.Parser[l.Type] then
                        continue
                    end
                    if self.Ignore[S] then
                        continue
                    end
                    table.insert(w.objects, self.Parser[l.Type].Save(S, l))
                end
                local S, z = pcall(o.JSONEncode, o, w)
                if not S then
                    return false, "failed to encode data"
                end
                if (not Q) then
                    writefile(N, z)
                end
                return true, z
            end
            function Z:Load(w)
                if (not w) then
                    return false, "no config file is selected"
                end
                if (type(w) == "table") then
                    for S, S in next, w.objects do
                        if self.Parser[S.type] then
                            task.spawn(function()
                                self.Parser[S.type].Load(S.idx, S)
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                    return true
                end
                const_0:CheckFolderTree()
                local S = self.Folder .. "/settings/" .. w .. ".json"
                if not i(S) then
                    return false, "invalid file"
                end
                local w, z = pcall(o.JSONDecode, o, readfile(S))
                if not w then
                    return false, "decode error"
                end
                for w, w in next, z.objects do
                    if self.Parser[w.type] then
                        task.spawn(function()
                            self.Parser[w.type].Load(w.idx, w)
                        end)
                    end
                end
                return true
            end
            function Z:Delete(w)
                if (not w) then
                    return false, "no config file is selected"
                end
                local S = self.Folder .. "/settings/" .. w .. ".json"
                if not i(S) then
                    return false, "invalid file"
                end
                local w, z = pcall(delfile, S)
                if not w then
                    return false, "delete file error"
                end
                return true
            end
            function Z:IgnoreThemeSettings()
                self:SetIgnoreIndexes({ "ThemeManager_ThemeList", "ThemeManager_CustomThemeList", "ThemeManager_CustomThemeName", "VideoLink", "Brightness", "Contrast", "Saturation", "Brightness", "BlurSize", "FontColor", "RiskColor", "MainColor", "AccentColor", "BackgroundColor", const_3[3][const_3[2]], "SwordColor", "ItemType", "BackTransparency", "BackColor", "SwordTransparency", "SwordReflectance", "SwordMaterial", "SwordSpeed" })
            end
            function Z:RefreshConfigList()
                local w = T(self.Folder .. "/settings")
                local S = {}
                for z = 1, #w do
                    local M = w[z]
                    if M:sub(-5) == ".json" then
                        local w = M:find(".json", 1, true)
                        local z = w
                        local o = M:sub(w, w)
                        while o ~= "/" and o ~= "\\" and o ~= "" do
                            w = w - 1
                            o = M:sub(w, w)
                        end
                        if o == "/" or o == "\\" then
                            table.insert(S, M:sub(w + 1, z - 1))
                        end
                    end
                end
                return S
            end
            function Z:SetLibrary(w)
                self.Library = w
            end
            function Z:LoadAutoloadConfig()
                const_0:CheckFolderTree()
                if i(self.Folder .. "/settings/autoload.txt") then
                    local w = readfile(self.Folder .. "/settings/autoload.txt")
                    local S, z = self:Load(w)
                    if not S then
                        return self.Library:Notify("Failed to load autoload config: " .. z)
                    end
                    if (not SILENT) then
                        self.Library:Notify(string.format("Auto loaded config %q", w))
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        getgenv().SaveManager = const_0
    end
end

} }
            function S:ApplyTheme(w)
                if (not w) then
                    return
                end
                local o = self:GetCustomTheme(w)
                local i = o or self.BuiltInThemes[w]
                if not i then
                    return
                end
                if self.Library.InnerVideoBackground ~= nil then
                    self.Library.InnerVideoBackground.Visible = false
                end
                local w = i[2]
                for i, T in next, o or w do
                    if i:find("Color") then
                        self.Library[i] = Color3.fromHex(T)
                        if const_0[i] then
                            const_0[i]:SetValueRGB(Color3.fromHex(T))
                        end
                    else
                        self.Library[i] = T
                        if const_0[i] then
                            const_0[i]:SetValue(T)
                        end
                    end
                end
                self:ThemeUpdate()
            end
            function S:ThemeUpdate()
                if self.Library.InnerVideoBackground ~= nil then
                    self.Library.InnerVideoBackground.Visible = false
                end
                local w = { "Contrast", "Saturation", "Brightness", "BlurSize", "FontColor", "RiskColor", "MainColor", "AccentColor", "BackgroundColor", z[3][z[2]], "SwordColor", "ItemType", "BackTransparency", "BackColor", "SwordTransparency", "SwordReflectance", "SwordMaterial", "SwordSpeed" }
                for o, o in next, w do
                    if const_0 and const_0[o] then
                        self.Library[o] = const_0[o].Value
                    end
                end
                self.Library.AccentColorDark = self.Library:GetDarkerColor(self.Library.AccentColor)
                self.Library:UpdateColorsUsingRegistry()
            end
            function S:LoadDefault()
                local w = "Default"
                local o = isfile(self.Folder .. "/themes/default.txt") and readfile(self.Folder .. "/themes/default.txt")
                local i = true
                if o then
                    if self.BuiltInThemes[o] then
                        w = o
                    elseif self:GetCustomTheme(o) then
                        w = o
                        i = false
                    end
                elseif self.BuiltInThemes[self.DefaultTheme] then
                    w = self.DefaultTheme
                end
                if i then
                    const_0.ThemeManager_ThemeList:SetValue(w)
                else
                    self:ApplyTheme(w)
                end
            end
            function S:SaveDefault(w)
                writefile(self.Folder .. "/themes/default.txt", w)
            end
            function S:Delete(w)
                if (not w) then
                    return false, "no config file is selected"
                end
                local o = self.Folder .. "/themes/" .. w
                if not isfile(o) then
                    return false, "invalid file"
                end
                local w, i = pcall(delfile, o)
                if not w then
                    return false, "delete file error"
                end
                return true
            end
            function S:GetCustomTheme(w)
                if (not w) then
                    return
                end
                local o = self.Folder .. "/themes/" .. w
                if not isfile(o) then
                    return nil
                end
                local w = readfile(o)
                local o, i = pcall(Z.JSONDecode, Z, w)
                if not o then
                    return nil
                end
                return i
            end
            function S:SaveCustomTheme(w)
                if not w or w:gsub(" ", "") == "" then
                    return self.Library:Notify("Invalid file name for theme (empty)", 3)
                end
                local o = {}
                local i = { "Contrast", "Saturation", "Brightness", "BlurSize", "FontColor", "RiskColor", "MainColor", "AccentColor", "BackgroundColor", z[3][z[2]], "ItemType", "SwordColor", "BackTransparency", "BackColor", "SwordTransparency", "SwordReflectance", "SwordMaterial", "SwordSpeed" }
                for z, z in next, i do
                    if (const_0[z]) then
                        if not z:find("Color") then
                            o[z] = const_0[z].Value
                        else
                            o[z] = const_0[z].Value:ToHex()
                        end
                    end
                end
                writefile(self.Folder .. "/themes/" .. w, Z:JSONEncode(o))
            end
            function S:ReloadCustomThemes()
                local w = listfiles(self.Folder .. "/themes")
                local z = {}
                for M = 1, #w do
                    local Z = w[M]
                    if Z:sub(-5) == ".json" then
                        local w = Z:find(".json", 1, true)
                        local M = Z:sub(w, w)
                        while M ~= "/" and M ~= "\\" and M ~= "" do
                            w = w - 1
                            M = Z:sub(w, w)
                        end
                        if M == "/" or M == "\\" then
                            table.insert(z, Z:sub(w + 1))
                        end
                    end
                end
                return z
            end
            function S:SetLibrary(w)
                self.Library = w
            end
            function S:BuildFolderTree()
                local w = {}
                local z = self.Folder:split("/")
                for M = 1, #z do
                    w[#w + 1] = table.concat(z, "/", 1, M)
                end
                table.insert(w, self.Folder .. "/themes")
                for z = 1, #w do
                    local M = w[z]
                    if not isfolder(M) then
                        makefolder(M)
                    end
                end
            end
            function S:SetFolder(w)
                self.Folder = w
                self:BuildFolderTree()
            end
            function S:CreateGroupBox(w)
                assert(self.Library, "Must set ThemeManager.Library first!")
                return w:AddLeftTabbox()
            end
            function S:ApplyToTab(w)
                assert(self.Library, "Must set ThemeManager.Library first!")
                local z = self:CreateGroupBox(w)
                self:CreateThemeManager(z)
            end
            function S:ApplyToGroupbox(w)
                assert(self.Library, "Must set ThemeManager.Library first!")
                self:CreateThemeManager(w)
            end
        end
        getgenv().ThemeManager = const_1
    end
end

local color_correction_module = function(constants)
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    return function()
        const_0.Enabled = Toggles.color_correction_enabled.Value
        if (not const_0.Enabled) then
            return
        end
        const_0.TintColor = Options.color_correction_tint.Value
        const_0.Saturation = Options.color_correction_saturation.Value
        const_0.Contrast = Options.color_correction_contrast.Value
        const_0.Brightness = Options.color_correction_brightness.Value
    end
end

local sunrays_module = function(constants)
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    return function()
        local sunrays_enabled = Toggles.sunrays_enabled.Value
        if (sunrays_enabled) then
            const_0.Enabled = true
            const_0.Spread = Options.sunrays_spread.Value
            const_0.Intensity = Options.sunrays_intensity.Value
        else
            for w, M in const_1 do
                const_0[w] = M
            end
        end
    end
end

local atmosphere_module = function(constants)
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    return function()
        const_0.Parent = Toggles.atmosphere_enabled.Value and game:GetService("Lighting") or nil
        if (not const_0.Parent) then
            return
        end
        const_0.Color = Options.atmosphere_color.Value
        const_0.Decay = Options.atmosphere_decay.Value
        const_0.Glare = Options.atmosphere_glare.Value
        const_0.Haze = Options.atmosphere_haze.Value
        const_0.Density = Options.atmosphere_density.Value
        const_0.Offset = Options.atmosphere_offset.Value

local bloom_module = function(constants)
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    return function()
        local bloom_enabled = Toggles.bloom_enabled.Value
        if (bloom_enabled) then
            const_0.Enabled = true
            const_0.Size = Options.bloom_size.Value
            const_0.Intensity = Options.bloom_intensity.Value
            const_0.Threshold = Options.bloom_threshold.Value
        else
            for w, M in const_1 do
                const_0[w] = M
            end
        end
    end
end

local muzzle_flash_module = function(constants)
    local const_10 = constants[10]
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_11 = constants[11]
    local const_7 = constants[7]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_12 = constants[12]
    local const_8 = constants[8]
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_9 = constants[9]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_6 = constants[6]
    return function(w, V, u)
        local a, _ = M("Attachment", o), M("Attachment", o)
        a.WorldPosition, _.WorldPosition = w, w
        local w = N:Clone()
        local M = Q.Value
        w.Width0, w.Width1 = M, M
        w.LightEmission = const_10.Value
        w.TextureSpeed = const_2.Value
        w.TextureLength = const_1.Value
        w.Parent = a
        w.Color = u
        w.Attachment0 = a
        w.Attachment1 = _
        local S = { w }
        for M = 1, (const_9.Value - 1) do
            local M = w:Clone()
            M.Parent = w.Parent
            table.insert(S, M)
        end
        const_12:Create(_, const_0(const_4.Value), { WorldPosition = V }):Play()
        task.delay(const_7.Value, function()
            local w
            for z, z in S do
                w = const_12:Create(z, const_0(const_6.Value), { Width0 = 0, Width1 = 0 })
                w:Play()
            end
            w.Completed:Wait()
            a:Destroy()
            _:Destroy()
        end)
    end
end

                Ts = const_1(5, const_7[Options.esp_font.Value].size - 2)
                do
                    local N, V = zs.images[1]._frame, A[Options.esp_fill_image.Value] or A.default
                    N.Image, N.BackgroundTransparency = V.image, V.image ~= "" and 1 or 0
                    f = V.size
                end
                local esp_flag_font = Options.esp_flag_font.Value
                r = const_8[esp_flag_font]
                const_3(zs.text[4], esp_flag_font)
                if (Zs and Zs.Parent) then
                    local S = Zs.Parent
                    if (not t and Ss._is_ally) then
                        pcall(const_18, Zs, "Parent", workspace)
                    else
                        pcall(const_18, Zs, "Parent", const_10)
                    end
                    Q:change(Zs)
                end
            end))
            Ms(const_17.events.esp_effects_changed.Event:Connect(function()
                for S, S in { "aura", "particle" } do
                    if (not t and Ss._is_ally) then
                        T:destroy(S)
                        continue
                    end
                    if (Zs and Toggles["esp_effects_" .. S].Value) then
                        T:create(Zs, Options["esp_effects_" .. S .. "_value"].Value, S)
                    else
                        T:destroy(S)
                    end
                end
            end))
            Ms(const_17.events.esp_effects_color_changed.Event:Connect(function()
                for S, S in { "aura", "particle" } do
                    T:update(S)
                end
            end))
            local S, S, S = math.cos, math.sin, math.atan2
            Ms(const_11.RenderStepped:Connect(function(S)
                if (not Y or not is or (not t and Ss._is_ally)) then
                    return C()
                end
                if (g <= 0) then
                    return C()
                end
                local Z = is.Position
                local N, l = Ps(Z)
                if (not N) then
                    return C()
                end
                H = false
                j()
                i(N, l)
                q(N, l)
                y(N, l, S)
                P(N, l, Z)
                w(N, l)
                _(N, l, Z)
                u(N, l, S)
            end))
            local w = function(S)
                Zs = S
                is = S:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", const_14)
                os = S:WaitForChild("Humanoid", const_14)
                Ms(os:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
                    g = os.Health
                end))
                Ms(os:GetPropertyChangedSignal("MaxHealth"):Connect(function()
                    k = os.MaxHealth
                end))
                g, k = os.Health, os.MaxHealth
                for Z, Z in O do
                    local i = S:WaitForChild(Z, z)
                    local z = i.Size / 2
                    local N, l, P = z.X, z.Y, z.Z
                    Ns[Z], ls[i] = i, { const_5(-N, -l, -P), const_5(-N, -l, P), const_5(-N, l, -P), const_5(-N, l, P), const_5(N, -l, -P), const_5(N, -l, P), const_5(N, l, -P), const_5(N, l, P) }
                end
                task.wait()
                if (not t and Ss._is_ally) then
                    return
                end
                pcall(function()
                    S.Parent = const_10
                end)
                Q:change(Zs)
                for S, S in { "aura", "particle" } do
                    if (Zs and Toggles["esp_effects_" .. S].Value) then
                        T:create(Zs, Options["esp_effects_" .. S .. "_value"].Value, S)
                    else
                        T:destroy(S)
                    end
                end
            end
            Ms(ws.CharacterAdded:Connect(w))
            Ms(ws.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function(S)
                if (Zs == S) then
                    Zs, is, os, g = nil, nil, nil, 0
                    zs.text[2].Text = "none"
                    table.clear(Ns)
                    table.clear(ls)
                end
            end))
            if (ws.Character) then
                task.spawn(w, ws.Character)
            end
            const_12.running[ws] = zs
        end
    end
end

local esp_text_render_module = function(constants)

    local const_11 = constants[11]

    local const_26 = constants[26]
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_15 = constants[15]
    local const_17 = constants[17]
    local const_20 = constants[20]
    local const_19 = constants[19]
    local const_13 = constants[13]
    local const_10 = constants[10]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_27 = constants[27]

            end

        else

        const_7.Size = const_5(const_10[3][const_10[2]], const_10[3][const_10[2]])

local esp_healthbar_module = function(constants)
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    return function()
        local w = ColorSequence.new
        local i = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new
        local T = Color3.fromHSV
        local Q = 3
        local N, l = {}, {}
        local P = 0
        local j, b = 0, 0.25
        local esp_healthbar_type, esp_healthbar_moving, esp_healthbar_speed = Options.esp_healthbar_type, Toggles.esp_healthbar_moving, Options.esp_healthbar_speed
        local _, A, H = V.Value, u.Value, a.Value
        local g = o.gradients.healthbar
        local o = function(C, k, v)
            local L = {}
            if (v == "rainbow") then
                for r = 0, 19 do
                    local D = r / 19
                    local r = D
                    if (k) then
                        r = r + P
                        if (r > 1) then
                            r = r - 1
                        end
                    end
                    L[#L + 1] = i(D, T(r, 0.5, 1))
                end
            elseif (v == "gradient") then
                local T = Q - 1
                local v = j / b
                for r = 0, T do
                    local D = #L + 1
                    local n = l[D]
                    local f = C[n]
                    if (k) then
                        local k = l[D - 1] or l[Q]
                        f = f:Lerp(C[k], v)
                    end
                    L[D] = i(r / T, f)
                end
            else
                L = C[1]
            end
            return L
        end
        local i = function()
            const_1[3][const_1[2]] = w(o(N, A, _))
            for T, T in g do
                T.Color = const_1[3][const_1[2]]
            end
        end
        const_2.events.gradient_option_changed.Event:Connect(function(T)
            table.clear(l)
            Q = T * 3
            for C, k in { [N] = { Options.esp_healthbar_color_start, Options.esp_healthbar_color_middle, Options.esp_healthbar_color_end } } do
                table.clear(C)
                for v, v in k do
                    for k = 1, T do
                        table.insert(C, v.Value)
                    end
                end
            end
            for T = 1, Q do
                table.insert(l, T)
            end
            i()
        end)
        V:OnChanged(function(i)
            _ = i
            const_2.events.gradient_option_changed:Fire(Options.esp_healthbar_slices.Value)
        end)
        u:OnChanged(function(i)
            A = i
            const_2.events.gradient_option_changed:Fire(Options.esp_healthbar_slices.Value)
        end)
        a:OnChanged(function(i)
            H = i
            const_2.events.gradient_option_changed:Fire(Options.esp_healthbar_slices.Value)
        end)
        const_2.events.gradient_option_changed:Fire(Options.esp_healthbar_slices.Value)
        const_4.RenderStepped:Connect(function(S)
            if (not A or not const_0[3][const_0[2]]) then
                return
            end
            S = S * Q / 3 * H
            P = P + S
            j = j + S
            if (P > 1) then
                P = 0
            end
            if (j > b) then
                j = 0
                local S = table.clone(l)
                if (A) then
                    l[1] = S[Q]
                    for z = 2, Q do
                        l[z] = S[z - 1]
                    end
                else
                    for S = 1, Q do
                        l[S] = S
                    end
                end
            end
            local S = w(o(N, H, _))
            const_1[3][const_1[2]] = S
            for w, w in g do
                w.Color = S
            end
        end)
    end
end

local esp_lerp_helper = function(constants)
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_10 = constants[10]
    local const_11 = constants[11]
    local const_9 = constants[9]
    local const_13 = constants[13]
    local const_6 = constants[6]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_12 = constants[12]
    local const_14 = constants[14]
    local const_8 = constants[8]
    local const_7 = constants[7]
    return function(w, a)
        if (not const_13[3][const_13[2]]) then
            return
        end
        local T = P.Value
        local P, _ = S.Value, z.Value
        local S = N.Value
        local z = ((Z(a * M.Value) + 1) / 2) * u.Value
        local M
        if (T > 0) then
            M = const_9((a * 360 * T) % 360)
        else
            M = const_9(S)
        end
        const_1[3][const_1[2]] = const_1[3][const_1[2]]:Lerp(w, const_14.Value)
        for w, S in const_12 do
            local i, T = S[1], S[2]
            local S = M + V * (w - 1)
            local w = o(Q(S), Z(S))
            local S = _ + z
            local z = P
            i.To = const_1[3][const_1[2]] + (w * S)
            i.From = const_1[3][const_1[2]] + (w * S) + (w * z)
            if (T) then
                S, z = S - 1, z + 2
                T.To = const_1[3][const_1[2]] + (w * S)
                T.From = const_1[3][const_1[2]] + (w * S) + (w * z)
            end
        end
    end
end

    local const_32 = constants[32]
    local const_9 = constants[9]
    local const_20 = constants[20]
    local const_16 = constants[16]
    local const_19 = constants[19]
    local const_28 = constants[28]
    local const_30 = constants[30]
    local const_26 = constants[26]
    local const_7 = constants[7]
    local const_12 = constants[12]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_8 = constants[8]
    local const_31 = constants[31]
    local const_15 = constants[15]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_13 = constants[13]
    local const_21 = constants[21]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_11 = constants[11]
    local const_6 = constants[6]
    local const_23 = constants[23]
    return function(w, y, q, K, F, B, U, p, d)
        const_16(8)
        local h, R = L.Value, r.Value
        local L = H.Value
        local H = C.Value
        local C, r
        local E, J = nil, nil
        if (q) then
            local t, Y = h["position on target"], R["position on target"]
            if (t or Y or L["position on target"] or H["position on target"]) then
                local c, e
                if (F) then
                    c, e = K, F
                else
                    c, e = const_10[3][const_10[2]](q.Position)
                end
                if (e) then
                    K, E, F = c, c, e
                    if (t) then
                        r = const_2.Position:Lerp(K, const_0.Value)
                    end
                    if (Y) then
                        C = const_9.Position:Lerp(K, const_18.Value)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local t = N[3][N[2]].EquippedItem
        if (t and t.ViewModel and t.Info.Type == "Gun") then
            local N, Y = not r and h["position on barrel"], not C and R["position on barrel"]
            if (N or Y or L["position on barrel"] or H["position on barrel"]) then
                local h = t.ViewModel
                if (h and (#h._muzzle_attachments > 0 or (h._body_model and h._body_model.PrimaryPart))) then
                    local R, c, e = pcall(function()
                        return const_10[3][const_10[2]](h:GetMuzzlePosition())
                    end)
                    const_16(8)
                    if (R and e) then
                        J = c
                        if (N) then
                            r = const_2.Position:Lerp(c, const_0.Value)
                        end
                        if (Y) then
                            C = const_9.Position:Lerp(c, const_18.Value)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if const_1[3][const_1[2]] then
                local N, u = l[3][l[2]], ""
                local l, D, h = t.Info.MaxAmmo, t.Data.AmmoReserve, t.Data.Ammo
                if ((l and l ~= const_6) and (D and D ~= const_6) and (h and h ~= const_6)) then
                    if (N["\"out of ammo\" if empty"] and h == 0 and D == 0) then
                        B = B .. const_11[3][const_11[2]]("\"out of ammo\" if empty", "out of ammo")
                    else
                        if (N.ammo) then
                            B = B .. const_11[3][const_11[2]]("ammo", h)
                            u = const_11[3][const_11[2]]("ammo divider", "/")
                        end
                        if (N["max ammo"]) then
                            B = B .. u .. const_11[3][const_11[2]]("max ammo", l)
                            u = const_11[3][const_11[2]]("ammo divider", "/")
                        end
                        if (N.reserve) then
                            B = B .. u .. const_11[3][const_11[2]]("reserve", D)
                        end
                    end
                    if (B ~= "") then
                        B = B .. "\n"
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if (not const_12) then
            const_12 = const_9.Position:Lerp(w, const_18.Value)
        end
        if (not const_15) then
            const_15 = const_2.Position:Lerp(w, const_0.Value)
        end
        local Z = (H["position on target"] and E) or (H["position on barrel"] and J) or w
        const_4[3][const_4[2]] = const_4[3][const_4[2]]:Lerp(Z, const_32.Value)
        const_19.Position = const_4[3][const_4[2]] + const_21[3][const_21[2]]
        const_19.Text = B
        local Z = x()
        const_33[3][const_33[2]]((const_31["position on target"] and E) or (const_31["position on barrel"] and J) or w, Z)
        const_2.Position, const_9.Position = const_15, const_12
        const_24.Position, const_8.Position = const_15, const_12
        if (const_30.Value) then
            local w = (Z * 360 * V.Value) % 360
            for M, M in const_3 do
                M.Rotation = w
            end
        end
        if (const_23.Value) then
            local w = (Z * 360 * o.Value) % 360
            for M, M in const_17 do
                M.Rotation = w
            end
        end
        if (q and F) then
            if (const_22.Value) then
                const_5.Adornee = y
                const_5.Enabled = true
            else
                const_5.Adornee = nil
                const_5.Enabled = false
            end
            if (const_28.Value) then
                const_7.Visible = true
                const_7.To = K or const_10[3][const_10[2]](q.Position)
                const_7.From = const_15
            else
                const_7.Visible = false
            end
        else
            const_7.Visible = false
            const_5.Adornee = nil
            const_5.Enabled = false
        end
        const_25[3][const_25[2]](U, p, d)
    end
end

local chat_spam_module = function(constants)
    local const_6 = constants[6]
    local const_5 = constants[5]
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    return function()
        if (const_5() < const_0[3][const_0[2]]) then
            return
        end
        const_0[3][const_0[2]] = const_5() + 5
        local w = Z.Value
        local z = i.default[w] or i.messages[w]
        if (not z or #z == 0) then
            return
        end
        local w = 1
        if (const_4.Value) then
            const_3[3][const_3[2]] = (const_3[3][const_3[2]] % #z) + 1
            w = const_3[3][const_3[2]]
        else
            w = math.random(1, #z)
        end
        local M = z[w]
        const_6:SendAsync(M)
    end
end

local target_part_finder_helper = function(constants)
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_4 = constants[4]
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    return function(w, i)
        local T = S
        local S, Q
        for N, l in i do
            if (const_3[N] or const_2[3][const_2[2]][N]) then
                continue
            elseif (const_0(l) ~= "Instance" or l.ClassName ~= "MeshPart") then
                continue
            end
            local z = Z[3][Z[2]](l.Position)
            local M = (z - w).Magnitude
            if (M < T) then
                T, S = M, l
                Q = z
            end
        end
        return S, Q
    end
end

    end
end

local tween_ease_helper = function(constants)
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    return function(w, z)
        return const_0(w * z * 120, 1)
    end
end

local interpolate_helper = function(constants)
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    return function(w, Z)
        local o = M(S, w, Z - w, z)
        return not o, o
    end
end

local lerp_helper = function(constants)
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    return function(w, Z, o)
        local i, T = S(Z, o), M(Z, o)
        return const_1(w, i, T)
    end
end

local forward_call_helper = function(constants)
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    return function(w, M, Z)
        return const_1(const_0, w, M, Z)
    end
end

local forward_helper = function(constants)
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    return function(...)
        const_0(const_1, ...)
    end
end

local call_helper = function()
    return function(...)
        return call(...)
    end
end

local call_helper_alt = function()
    return function(...)
        return call(...)
    end
end

local noop_helper = function()
    return function()
    end
end

local apply_font_helper = function(constants)
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    return function(w, z)
        local M = S[z]
        if (not M) then
            return
        end
        w.TextSize, w.FontFace = M.size, M.font
    end
end

local aim_direction_check_helper = function(constants)
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    return function(w)
        local o = z[S]
        return const_1((const_2(0, o.Y, 0) * const_2(o.X, 0, 0)).LookVector, w.Unit) > 0.5
    end
end

local tracer_position_helper = function(constants)
    local const_3 = constants[3]
    local const_2 = constants[2]
    local const_1 = constants[1]
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    return function()
        if (const_0.Value) then
            local w = S.CFrame
            const_1._current.Position = w.Position + const_2
        end
    end
end

local config_preset_data = function(constants)
    local const_0 = constants[0]
    return function()

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local utility = require(Modules:WaitForChild("utility"))

local Replication = Remotes:WaitForChild("Replication")
local FighterRemote = Remotes:WaitForChild("Fighter")
local UseItemRemote = FighterRemote:WaitForChild("UseItem")

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local Modules = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules"))

-- ============================================================================

-- RAGEBOT

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local Modules = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules"))
local CONSTANTS = require(Modules:WaitForChild("CONSTANTS"))
local utility = require(Modules:WaitForChild("utility"))
local ClientFighter = require(LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Controllers.ClientFighter)

local Remotes = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes"))
local Replication = Remotes:WaitForChild("Replication")
local FighterRemote = Remotes:WaitForChild("Fighter")
local UseItemRemote = FighterRemote:WaitForChild("UseItem")

local IS_PRIVATE_SERVER = game.PlaceId == CONSTANTS.IS_PRIVATE_SERVER

local Ragebot = {}
Ragebot.__index = Ragebot

local function randomUnitVector()
    return Random.new():NextUnitVector()
end

local function generateGUID()
    return HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)
end

function Ragebot.new()
    local self = setmetatable({}, Ragebot)

    self.characters = {}
    self.duelCache = {}
    self.priorities = {}
    self.voidedPlayers = {}

    self.attackNum = 0
    self.heavyAttackNum = 0
    self.reloadCooldown = 0
    self.lastAttack = 0
    self.attackThread = nil
    self.reloadThreads = {}

    self.desync = {
        enabled = false,
        pitch = 0,
        yaw = 0,
        customPitch = 0,
        customYaw = 0,
        floorHide = false,
        physicsRep = nil,
    }

    self.status = {
        enabled = false,
        text = "",
        weapon = nil,
    }

    self.events = {
        force_cframe_change = Instance.new("BindableEvent"),
        ragebot_status = Instance.new("BindableEvent"),
    }

    self:trackCharacters()
    self:setupValidation()
    self:setupAntiAim()
    self:setupVoidPosition()
    self:setupVoidSpam()
    self:setupPriorityTargeting()
    self:setupIndicator()

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not Toggles.ragebot_enabled.Value then
            self.status.enabled = false
            return
        end
        self.status.enabled = true
    end)

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if Toggles.ragebot_enabled.Value and Toggles.ragebot_void_spam.Value then
            if self.attackThread and coroutine.status(self.attackThread) ~= "dead" then
                return
            end
            self.attackThread = task.spawn(function()
                self:voidLoop()
            end)
        end
    end)

    return self
end

function Ragebot:trackCharacters()
    local function add(plr, char)
        self.characters[plr] = char
        self.duelCache[plr] = cloneref(char)
    end
    local function remove(plr)
        self.characters[plr] = nil
        self.duelCache[plr] = nil
        self.priorities[plr] = nil
        self.voidedPlayers[plr] = nil
    end
    local function hook(plr)
        if plr.Character then add(plr, plr.Character) end
        plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char) add(plr, char) end)
        plr.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function() remove(plr) end)
    end
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do hook(plr) end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(hook)
end

function Ragebot:setupValidation()
    local function hasFlag(char, name)
        local flag = char and char:FindFirstChild(name)
        return flag ~= nil
    end

    function self:isAlly(char)
        local flag = char and char:FindFirstChild("_is_ally")
        return flag ~= nil and flag.Value == true
    end

    function self:isAlive(char)
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        return hum ~= nil and hum.Health > 0
    end

    function self:getClientFighter(char)
        local client = char and char:FindFirstChild("ClientFighter")
        return client or (char and char:GetAttribute("client_fighter"))
    end

    function self:getHitbox(char)
        local head = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HitboxHead") or char:FindFirstChild("Head"))
        return head or (char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
    end

    function self:validateTarget(char)
        if not char then return false end
        if self:isAlly(char) then return false end
        if not self:isAlive(char) then return false end
        if not self:getClientFighter(char) then return false end
        if not hasFlag(char, "Health") then return false end
        return true
    end
end

function Ragebot:setupAntiAim()
    local self = self

    function self:applyDesync(cframe, hrp, rep)
        if rep then
            rep.CFrame = cframe
        end
        if hrp then
            self.events.force_cframe_change:Fire(hrp, cframe)
        end
    end

    function self:unbindAntiAim()
        pcall(function()
            RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RagebotAntiAim")
        end)
    end

    function self:desyncCFrame(hrp)
        local base = hrp.CFrame
        local yaw = math.rad(self.desync.yaw)
        local pitch = math.rad(self.desync.pitch)
        local customYaw = math.rad(self.desync.customYaw)
        local customPitch = math.rad(self.desync.customPitch)
        return CFrame.new(base.Position)
            * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw + customYaw, 0)
            * CFrame.Angles(pitch + customPitch, 0, 0)
    end

    RunService:BindToRenderStep("RagebotAntiAim", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value, function()
        if not Toggles.ragebot_enabled.Value then
            self:unbindAntiAim()
            return
        end
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not character then return end
        local hrp = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end

        self.desync.enabled = Toggles.anti_aim_enabled.Value
        self.desync.pitch = Options.anti_aim_pitch.Value
        self.desync.yaw = Options.anti_aim_yaw.Value
        self.desync.customPitch = Options.anti_aim_custom_pitch.Value
        self.desync.customYaw = Options.anti_aim_custom_yaw.Value
        self.desync.floorHide = Toggles.anti_aim_floor_hide.Value

        local rep = character:FindFirstChild("PhysicsRepRootPart") or hrp
        self.desync.physicsRep = rep

        if not self.desync.enabled then
            rep.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
            return
        end

        if self.desync.floorHide and hrp.Position.Y < 8 then
            self:applyDesync(hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -3, 0), hrp, rep)
            return
        end

        self:applyDesync(self:desyncCFrame(hrp), hrp, rep)
    end)
end

function Ragebot:setupVoidPosition()
    local self = self
    local cache = {}

    local function collectBarriers()
        local barriers = {}
        local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
        local barrierFolder = map and map:FindFirstChild("Barriers")
        if barrierFolder then
            for _, part in ipairs(barrierFolder:GetChildren()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    table.insert(barriers, part)
                end
            end
        end
        return barriers
    end

    local function collectSpawns()
        local positions = {}
        local spawns = workspace:FindFirstChild("Spawns")
        if not spawns then return positions end
        for _, obj in ipairs(spawns:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("Model") then
                local pos = obj:FindFirstChild("Position")
                if pos and pos:IsA("Vector3Value") then
                    table.insert(positions, pos.Value)
                end
            elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                table.insert(positions, obj.Position)
            end
        end
        return positions
    end

    function self:getVoidPosition()
        local now = tick()
        if cache.expire and now < cache.expire and cache.position then
            return cache.position
        end

        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = collectBarriers()
        params.BruteForceAllSlow = true

        local candidates = collectSpawns()
        for _, origin in ipairs(candidates) do
            local from = origin + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
            local result = workspace:Raycast(from, Vector3.new(0, -500, 0), params)
            if result then
                local hit = result.Position
                if hit.Y < 0 then
                    cache.position = hit
                    cache.expire = now + 1
                    return hit
                end
            end
        end

        if cache.position then
            return cache.position
        end
        return nil
    end

    function self:voidPositionOrFallback()
        return self:getVoidPosition() or (LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
    end
end

function Ragebot:setupVoidSpam()
    local self = self

    local function getEquippedItem(character)
        return character and character:FindFirstChild("EquippedItem")
    end

    local function getRotationCFrame(part)
        return part and part.CFrame.Rotation or CFrame.new()
    end

    local function getAnimationKeys(item)
        local viewModel = item and item.ViewModel
        return viewModel and viewModel:GetAnimationKeys() or {}
    end

    local function hasRiotShield(character)
        local item = getEquippedItem(character)
        return item and item.Name == "Riot Shield" or false
    end

    local function equipItem(character, itemName)
        return pcall(function()
            local item = getEquippedItem(character)
            if item and item.EquipItem then
                item:EquipItem(itemName)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function fireUseItem(payload)
        return pcall(function()
            UseItemRemote:FireServer(payload)
        end)
    end

    function self:voidSpamMelee(target)
        local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
        local item = getEquippedItem(myChar)
        if not item then return false end
        if item.Info and item.Info.Type ~= "Melee" then return false end

        local now = tick()
        local cooldown = item.Info and item.Info.HeavyAttackCooldown or 0.2
        if now - self.lastAttack < cooldown then
            return false
        end
        self.lastAttack = now

        self.heavyAttackNum = self.heavyAttackNum + 1

        local hitbox = self:getHitbox(target)
        if not hitbox then return false end

        local aimPos = hitbox.Position + randomUnitVector() * 0.35
        local rotation = getRotationCFrame(hasRiotShield(target) and hitbox or target)

        local ok = fireUseItem({
            id = generateGUID(),
            objectId = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false),
            item = Options.ragebot_weapon.Value,
            position = aimPos,
            heavyAttackNum = self.heavyAttackNum,
            animationKeys = getAnimationKeys(item),
            rotation = rotation,
            one = Vector3.one,
            startAiming = true,
            packed = { "\x00", "\x01", "\x02", "\x03" },
        })

        if ok then
            pcall(function()
                item:StartAiming(aimPos)
            end)
            pcall(function()
                item:HeavyAttack(aimPos)
            end)
            self.status.text = string.format("stabbing %s in %s... (void)", target.Name, tostring(aimPos))
        else
            self.status.text = "void... (backstabbing)"
        end
        return ok
    end

    function self:voidSpamRanged(target)
        local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
        local item = getEquippedItem(myChar)
        if not item then return false end
        if item.Info and item.Info.Type ~= "Gun" then return false end

        self.attackNum = self.attackNum + 1

        local hitbox = self:getHitbox(target)
        if not hitbox then return false end

        local aimPos = hitbox.Position + randomUnitVector() * 0.5
        local rotation = getRotationCFrame(hasRiotShield(target) and hitbox or target)

        local ok = fireUseItem({
            id = generateGUID(),
            objectId = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false),
            item = Options.ragebot_weapon.Value,
            position = aimPos,
            attackNum = self.attackNum,
            animationKeys = getAnimationKeys(item),
            rotation = rotation,
            one = Vector3.one,
            packed = { "\x00", "\x01", "\x02", "\x03" },
        })

        if ok then
            pcall(function()
                item:StartShooting(aimPos)
            end)
            self.status.text = "void spamming... (attacking " .. target.Name .. ")"
        else
            self.status.text = "void..."
        end
        return ok
    end

    function self:voidLoop()
        if not Toggles.ragebot_enabled.Value then return end
        if not Toggles.ragebot_void_spam.Value then return end

        local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not myChar then return end

        local item = getEquippedItem(myChar)
        if not item then
            self.status.text = "no usable item(s)... (void)"
            equipItem(myChar, Options.ragebot_weapon.Value)
            return
        end

        local target = self:getPriorityTarget()
        if not target then return end

        local weaponType = item.Info and item.Info.Type
        local data = item.Data
        local ammo = data and data.Ammo
        local reserve = data and data.AmmoReserve
        local now = tick()

        if weaponType == "Gun" then
            if ammo and ammo <= 0 then
                if reserve and reserve > 0 then
                    if now > self.reloadCooldown then
                        self.status.text = "reloading..."
                        pcall(function()
                            item:StartReloading()
                        end)
                        self.reloadCooldown = now + 0.4
                        table.insert(self.reloadThreads, tick())
                    end
                    return
                end
                local settings = Options.ragebot_settings.Value or {}
                if settings["swap weapons when empty"] then
                    equipItem(myChar, Options.ragebot_weapon.Value)
                    self.status.text = "swap weapons when empty"
                end
                return
            end

            if item.Name == "Revolver" then
                self.attackNum = self.attackNum + 1
                local hitbox = self:getHitbox(target)
                local aimPos = hitbox.Position + randomUnitVector() * 0.5
                fireUseItem({
                    id = generateGUID(),
                    item = Options.ragebot_weapon.Value,
                    position = aimPos,
                    attackNum = self.attackNum,
                    animationKeys = getAnimationKeys(item),
                    rotation = getRotationCFrame(hitbox),
                })
                self.status.text = "killing " .. target.Name .. " (Revolver)"
                return
            end

            self:voidSpamRanged(target)
            return
        end

        if weaponType == "Melee" then
            self:voidSpamMelee(target)
            return
        end

        equipItem(myChar, Options.ragebot_weapon.Value)
        self.status.text = "void..."
    end

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not Toggles.ragebot_enabled.Value then return end
        if not Toggles.ragebot_void_spam.Value then return end
        local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not myChar then return end
        local item = getEquippedItem(myChar)
        if not item then return end
        local weaponType = item.Info and item.Info.Type
        if weaponType ~= "Gun" and weaponType ~= "Melee" then
            self.status.text = "no usable item(s)... (void)"
        end
    end)
end

function Ragebot:setupPriorityTargeting()
    local self = self

    function self:addPriority(player, level)
        self.priorities[player] = level
        if Toggles.ragebot_priority_notification.Value then
            Library:Notify("added " .. player.Name .. " to ragebot priority", 2)
        end
    end

    function self:removePriority(player)
        self.priorities[player] = nil
    end

    function self:markVoided(player)
        self.voidedPlayers[player] = true
    end

    function self:unmarkVoided(player)
        self.voidedPlayers[player] = nil
    end

    function self:getPriorityTarget()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local origin = cam and cam.CFrame.Position
        if not origin then return nil end

        local best, bestScore = nil, math.huge
        for player, char in pairs(self.characters) do
            if player ~= LocalPlayer then
                if not self:validateTarget(char) then continue end
                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then
                    local dist = (hrp.Position - origin).Magnitude
                    local priority = self.priorities[player] or 0
                    local weight = 500
                    if priority > 0 then
                        weight = weight + priority * 100
                    end
                    local score = dist - weight
                    if self.voidedPlayers[player] then
                        score = score - 10000
                    end
                    if score < bestScore then
                        bestScore = score
                        best = char
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return best
    end
end

function Ragebot:setupIndicator()
    local self = self
    local lastUpdate = 0

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local now = tick()
        if now - lastUpdate < 0.1 then return end
        lastUpdate = now

        self.status.enabled = Toggles.ragebot_enabled.Value
        if not self.status.enabled then
            self.status.text = ""
            return
        end

        local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not myChar then return end
        local item = myChar:FindFirstChild("EquippedItem")
        local weaponType = item and item.Info and item.Info.Type

        if weaponType == "Gun" then
            self.status.text = "ragebot"
            self.status.weapon = item.Name
        elseif weaponType == "Melee" then
            local name = item.Name or "Knife"
            self.status.text = "ragebot: " .. name
            self.status.weapon = item
        else
            self.status.text = "invalid weapons for ragebot"
            self.status.weapon = nil
        end

        if Toggles.indicator_ragebot.Value then
            local style = Options.indicator_ragebot_style.Value
            self.events.ragebot_status:Fire(self.status.text)
        end
    end)
end

function Ragebot:setPriority(player, level)
    return self:addPriority(player, level)
end

function Ragebot:getState()
    return {
        enabled = self.status.enabled,
        text = self.status.text,
        weapon = self.status.weapon,
        priorityTarget = self:getPriorityTarget(),
    }
end

function Ragebot:getPriorityList()
    local list = {}
    for player, level in pairs(self.priorities) do
        table.insert(list, { player = player, priority = level })
    end
    return list
end

function Ragebot:getVoidedPlayers()
    local list = {}
    for player in pairs(self.voidedPlayers) do
        table.insert(list, player)
    end
    return list
end

-- ============================================================================

-- TRIGGERBOT

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local Triggerbot = {}
Triggerbot.__index = Triggerbot

local HITBOX_WHITELIST = {
    HitboxBody = true,
    HitboxHead = true,
    PhysicalHitboxHead = true,
    HitboxBodySmall = true,
    HitboxHeadSmall = true,
    FakeMass = true,
}

function Triggerbot.new(clientFighter, clientItem, input)
    local self = setmetatable({}, Triggerbot)
    self.clientFighter = clientFighter
    self.clientItem = clientItem
    self.input = input or (clientItem and clientItem.Input)

    self.rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
    self.rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

    self.lastTargetTime = 0
    self.lastTarget = nil
    self.nextTargetDelay = 0

    local function addToFilter(filter, container)
        if container and container.Parent then
            table.insert(filter, container)
        end
        return filter
    end

    local function buildFilter()
        local filter = {}
        addToFilter(filter, workspace:FindFirstChild("characters"))
        addToFilter(filter, workspace:FindFirstChild("viewmodels"))
        addToFilter(filter, LocalPlayer.Character)
        self.rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter
        self.rayParams.CollisionGroup = "NoCollide"
        return filter
    end

    local function getEquippedItem(character)
        return character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or (character and character:FindFirstChild("EquippedItem"))
    end

    local function isScopedAndAiming(item)
        if not item then return false end
        local scoped = Options.triggerbot_scoped.Value or {}
        for weaponName, enabled in pairs(scoped) do
            if enabled and (item.Name == weaponName or (item.Info and item.Info.Name == weaponName)) then
                local stateOk, state = pcall(function()
                    return item:GetState()
                end)
                if stateOk and (state == Enum.ItemState.Equipped or state == Enum.ItemState.Active) then
                    local aiming = item.IsFullyAiming and pcall(function()
                        return item:IsFullyAiming()
                    end) or false
                    if aiming then
                        return true
                    end
                end
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function rayToTarget()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return nil end

        buildFilter()

        local origin = cam.CFrame.Position
        local direction = cam.CFrame.LookVector
        local maxDistance = Options.triggerbot_max_distance.Value

        local result = workspace:Raycast(origin, direction * maxDistance, self.rayParams)
        if not result then return nil end

        local instance = result.Instance
        if not instance then return nil end

        local blacklist = Options.triggerbot_part_blacklist.Value or {}
        if blacklist[instance.Name] then
            return nil
        end

        if not HITBOX_WHITELIST[instance.Name] then
            return nil
        end

        local character = instance:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        if not character then return nil end

        local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return nil end

        local ally = character:FindFirstChild("_is_ally")
        if ally and ally.Value then return nil end

        local player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character)
        if player == LocalPlayer then return nil end

        return {
            part = instance,
            character = character,
            player = player,
            position = result.Position,
        }
    end

    local function antiKatanaCheck(character)
        local settings = Options.triggerbot_settings.Value or {}
        if not settings["anti katana"] then return true end
        local equipped = getEquippedItem(character)
        if not equipped then return true end
        local info = equipped.Info
        if not info then return true end
        local itemType = info.Type
        return itemType ~= "Melee" and equipped.Name ~= "Katana"
    end

    local function shouldShoot()
        if not Toggles.triggerbot_enabled.Value then return false end
        if not self:keyHeld() then return false end

        local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
        local item = getEquippedItem(myChar)
        if not item then return false end

        local info = item.Info
        local itemType = info and info.Type
        local settings = Options.triggerbot_settings.Value or {}

        if itemType == "Gun" then
            local canAim = item.IsFullyAiming and item:IsFullyAiming() or false
            if settings["scoped only"] and not canAim then
                return false
            end
        end

        local now = tick()
        local forgetSeconds = forgetTime
        if self.lastTarget and now - self.lastTargetTime > forgetSeconds then
            self.lastTarget = nil
        end

        if settings["no delay between targets"] then
            self.nextTargetDelay = 0
        end

        return true
    end

    local function tryShoot()
        if not shouldShoot() then return end

        local target = rayToTarget()
        if not target then return end

        local now = tick()
        if self.lastTarget and target.character ~= self.lastTarget then
            if now - self.lastTargetTime < self.nextTargetDelay then
                return
            end
        end
        self.lastTarget = target.character
        self.lastTargetTime = now

        if not antiKatanaCheck(target.character) then
            return
        end

        local reaction = Options.triggerbot_reaction_time.Value
        local offset = Options.triggerbot_reaction_time_offset.Value
        local shootDelay = Options.triggerbot_shoot_delay.Value

        task.delay((reaction + offset) / 1000, function()
            task.wait(shootDelay / 1000)
            if not Toggles.triggerbot_enabled.Value then return end
            if not self:keyHeld() then return end

            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                if self.clientItem then
                    self.clientItem:StartShooting(target.position)
                elseif self.input then
                    self.input(nil)
                end
            end)
            if not ok then
                pcall(function()
                    if self.input then
                        self.input(nil)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    function self:keyHeld()
        local mode = Toggles.triggerbot_keybind.Mode
        local key = Toggles.triggerbot_keybind.Value
        if mode == "Always" then
            return true
        end
        if mode == "Hold" then
            return UserInputService:IsKeyDown(key)
        end
        if mode == "Toggle" then
            return Toggles.triggerbot_keybind.KeyDown
        end
        return true
    end

    local forgetTime = Options.triggerbot_forget_time.Value
    Options.triggerbot_forget_time.OnChanged:Connect(function(value)
        forgetTime = value
    end)

    local function onHeartbeat()
        tryShoot()
    end

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(onHeartbeat)
    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        buildFilter()
    end)

    Options.triggerbot_scoped.OnChanged:Connect(function() end)
    Options.triggerbot_reaction_time.OnChanged:Connect(function() end)
    Options.triggerbot_reaction_time_offset.OnChanged:Connect(function() end)
    Options.triggerbot_max_distance.OnChanged:Connect(function() end)
    Options.triggerbot_shoot_delay.OnChanged:Connect(function() end)
    Options.triggerbot_settings.OnChanged:Connect(function() end)
    Options.triggerbot_part_blacklist.OnChanged:Connect(function() end)

    return self
end

-- ============================================================================

-- SILENT AIM

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local SilentAim = {}
SilentAim.__index = SilentAim

local FONT_NAMES = {
    "monocraft",
    "monocraft bold",
    "ubuntu",
    "tahoma",
    "proggy clean",
    "proggy tiny",
    "pixel arial",
    "smallest pixel",
}

local FONT_SIZES = {
    ["smallest pixel"] = 8,
    ["proggy tiny"] = 10,
    ["pixel arial"] = 11,
}

local DEFAULT_FONT = Enum.Font.Ubuntu

local function fontFor(name)
    if not name then return DEFAULT_FONT end
    local lower = string.lower(name)
    if string.find(lower, "monocraft") then return DEFAULT_FONT end
    if string.find(lower, "ubuntu") then return Enum.Font.Ubuntu end
    if string.find(lower, "tahoma") then return Enum.Font.Tahoma end
    if string.find(lower, "proggy") then return Enum.Font.Code end
    if string.find(lower, "arial") then return Enum.Font.Arial end
    if string.find(lower, "pixel") then return Enum.Font.Arial end
    return DEFAULT_FONT
end

local function applyFont(drawing, fontName, size)
    pcall(function()
        drawing.Font = fontFor(fontName)
    end)
    local fontSize = FONT_SIZES[fontName] or size or 12
    pcall(function()
        drawing.Size = fontSize
    end)
    return fontSize
end

local function textBounds(text, font, size)
    local params = Instance.new("GetTextBoundsParams")
    params.Text = text
    params.Size = size or 12
    params.Font = font or DEFAULT_FONT
    local ok, bounds = pcall(function()
        return TextService:GetTextBoundsAsync(params)
    end)
    return ok and bounds or Vector2.new(size or 12, size or 12)
end

local function makeDrawing(kind)
    return Drawing.new(kind)
end

function SilentAim.new(events)
    local self = setmetatable({}, SilentAim)
    self.events = events or {}
    self.drawings = {}
    self.fonts = {}
    self.onCloseCallbacks = {}

    self.fovCircle = makeDrawing("Circle")
    self.fovCircle.Thickness = 1
    self.fovCircle.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    self.fovCircle.Filled = false
    self.fovCircle.Visible = false

    self.fovFill = makeDrawing("Circle")
    self.fovFill.Thickness = 1
    self.fovFill.Filled = true
    self.fovFill.Visible = false

    self.fovOutline = makeDrawing("Circle")
    self.fovOutline.Thickness = 2
    self.fovOutline.Filled = false
    self.fovOutline.Visible = false

    self.targetLine = makeDrawing("Line")
    self.targetLine.Thickness = 1
    self.targetLine.Visible = false

    self.visualizeLine = makeDrawing("Line")
    self.visualizeLine.Thickness = 1
    self.visualizeLine.Visible = false

    table.insert(self.drawings, self.fovCircle)
    table.insert(self.drawings, self.fovFill)
    table.insert(self.drawings, self.fovOutline)
    table.insert(self.drawings, self.targetLine)
    table.insert(self.drawings, self.visualizeLine)

    self.target = nil
    self.manipulated = false
    self.hitchanceFail = false
    self.lastTargetChange = 0

    local function rebuildGradients(prefix, target)
        local gradients = target or {}
        local options = {}
        local index = 1
        while true do
            local option = Options[prefix .. index]
            if not option then break end
            table.insert(options, option)
            index = index + 1
        end
        if #options == 0 then
            return gradients
        end
        local keypoints = {}
        for i, option in ipairs(options) do
            local t = (#options > 1) and ((i - 1) / (#options - 1)) or 0
            table.insert(keypoints, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(t, option.Color))
        end
        if gradients.color then
            gradients.color = ColorSequence.new(keypoints)
        end
        if gradients.transparency then
            local trans = {}
            for i, option in ipairs(options) do
                local t = (#options > 1) and ((i - 1) / (#options - 1)) or 0
                table.insert(trans, NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(t, option.Transparency))
            end
            gradients.transparency = NumberSequence.new(trans)
        end
        return gradients
    end

    self.gradients = {
        silent = { color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1)), transparency = NumberSequence.new(0) },
        silent_fill = { color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1)), transparency = NumberSequence.new(0) },
        silent_outline = { color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(0, 0, 0)), transparency = NumberSequence.new(0) },
    }

    local function updateSilentGradients()
        self.gradients.silent = rebuildGradients("silent_color", self.gradients.silent)
        self.gradients.silent_fill = rebuildGradients("silent_fill_color", self.gradients.silent_fill)
        self.gradients.silent_outline = rebuildGradients("silent_outline_color", self.gradients.silent_outline)
        local rot = Options.silent_fov_rotation.Value
        self.fovCircle.Color = Options.silent_color1.Value
        self.fovFill.Color = Options.silent_fill_color1.Value
        self.fovOutline.Color = Options.silent_outline_color1.Value
    end

    local function espColorChanged()
        updateSilentGradients()
    end

    if self.events then
        if self.events.esp_color_changed then
            self.events.esp_color_changed.Event:Connect(espColorChanged)
        end
        if self.events.esp_fill_changed then
            self.events.esp_fill_changed.Event:Connect(updateSilentGradients)
        end
        if self.events.gradient_option_changed then
            self.events.gradient_option_changed.Event:Connect(function()
                updateSilentGradients()
            end)
        end
        if self.events.esp_remove_all then
            self.events.esp_remove_all.Event:Connect(function()
                self:destroy()
            end)
        end
        if self.events.on_close then
            self.events.on_close.Event:Connect(function()
                for _, callback in ipairs(self.onCloseCallbacks) do
                    pcall(callback)
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    local function getTargetPart(character)
        if Toggles.silent_closest_part.Value then
            local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
            for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                    local dist = (part.Position - cam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                    if dist < bestDist then
                        bestDist = dist
                        best = part
                    end
                end
            end
            return best
        end
        return character:FindFirstChild("HitboxHead")
            or character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    function self:acquire()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return nil end

        local radius = Options.silent_radius.Value
        local origin = cam.CFrame.Position
        local best, bestScore = nil, math.huge

        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player == LocalPlayer then continue end
            local character = player.Character
            if not character then continue end
            local hum = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then continue end
            local ally = character:FindFirstChild("_is_ally")
            if ally and ally.Value then continue end

            local part = getTargetPart(character)
            if not part then continue end

            local screen, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
            if not onScreen then continue end

            local distance = (part.Position - origin).Magnitude
            if distance > radius then continue end

            local viewport = cam.ViewportSize
            local center = Vector2.new(viewport.X / 2, viewport.Y / 2)
            local score = (Vector2.new(screen.X, screen.Y) - center).Magnitude
            if score < bestScore then
                bestScore = score
                best = {
                    player = player,
                    character = character,
                    part = part,
                    position = part.Position,
                    distance = distance,
                }
            end
        end
        self.target = best
        return best
    end

    local function updateFov()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end
        local radius = Options.silent_radius.Value
        local lerp = Options.silent_fov_lerp.Value

        local viewport = cam.ViewportSize
        local focal = (viewport.X / 2) / math.tan(math.rad(cam.FieldOfView) / 2)
        local pixelRadius = focal * math.tan(math.rad(radius))

        local center = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(cam.CFrame.Position)
        local centerVec = Vector2.new(center.X, center.Y)

        local circle = self.fovCircle
        circle.Radius = circle.Radius + (pixelRadius - circle.Radius) * lerp
        circle.Position = centerVec
        circle.Color = Options.silent_color1.Value

        local fill = self.fovFill
        fill.Radius = circle.Radius
        fill.Position = centerVec
        fill.Color = Options.silent_fill_color1.Value

        local outline = self.fovOutline
        outline.Radius = circle.Radius + 1
        outline.Position = centerVec
        outline.Color = Options.silent_outline_color1.Value

        local rotationSpeed = Options.silent_fov_rotation_speed.Value
        local rotation = Options.silent_fov_rotation.Value
        if Toggles.silent_fov_moving.Value then
            rotation = rotation + rotationSpeed * tick()
        end
    end

    local function onRender()
        local toggle = Toggles.silent_toggle.Value
        local showFov = Toggles.silent_showfov.Value
        local visualize = Toggles.silent_visualize.Value

        if not toggle then
            self.fovCircle.Visible = false
            self.fovFill.Visible = false
