            self.fovOutline.Visible = false
            self.targetLine.Visible = false
            self.visualizeLine.Visible = false
            return
        end

        updateFov()

        self.fovCircle.Visible = showFov
        self.fovFill.Visible = showFov and Toggles.silent_fov_fill.Value
        self.fovOutline.Visible = showFov and Toggles.silent_fov_outline.Value

        local target = self:acquire()
        if target and visualize then
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local screen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(target.position)
            local center = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(cam.CFrame.Position)
            local line = self.visualizeLine
            line.From = Vector2.new(center.X, center.Y)
            line.To = Vector2.new(screen.X, screen.Y)
            line.Color = Options.silent_color2.Value
            line.Visible = true
        else
            self.visualizeLine.Visible = false
        end
    end

    function self:getTarget()
        if not Toggles.silent_toggle.Value then return nil end
        local target = self:acquire()
        if not target then return nil end

        local hitchance = Options.silent_hitchance.Value
        if math.random(100) > hitchance then
            self.hitchanceFail = true
            self.manipulated = false
            return nil
        end
        self.hitchanceFail = false
        self.manipulated = true
        return target
    end

    function self:manipulate(originalPosition, originalPart, originalRay)
        if not Toggles.silent_manipulation.Value then
            self.manipulated = false
            return originalPosition, originalPart, originalRay
        end
        local target = self:getTarget()
        if not target then
            return nil, nil, nil
        end
        return target.position, target.part, nil
    end

    function self:onClose(callback)
        table.insert(self.onCloseCallbacks, callback)
    end

    function self:destroy()
        for _, drawing in ipairs(self.drawings) do
            pcall(function()
                drawing:Remove()
            end)
        end
        self.drawings = {}
    end

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(onRender)
    updateSilentGradients()

    return self
end

-- ============================================================================

-- AIMBOT

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local Aimbot = {}
Aimbot.__index = Aimbot

function Aimbot.new(targeting)
    local self = setmetatable({}, Aimbot)
    self.targeting = targeting or {}
    self.target = nil
    self.targetData = nil
    self.manipulated = false
    self.lastTick = tick()

    local fovCircle = Drawing.new("Circle")
    fovCircle.Thickness = 1
    fovCircle.Transparency = 1
    fovCircle.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    fovCircle.Filled = false
    fovCircle.Visible = false

    local fovFill = Drawing.new("Circle")
    fovFill.Thickness = 1
    fovFill.Transparency = 1
    fovFill.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    fovFill.Filled = true
    fovFill.Visible = false

    local function isAlly(character)
        local flag = character and character:FindFirstChild("_is_ally")
        return flag ~= nil and flag.Value == true
    end

    local function isFlashbanged(character)
        local flash = character and character:FindFirstChild("Flashbang")
        return flash ~= nil
    end

    local function getClientFighter(character)
        return character and character:FindFirstChild("ClientFighter")
    end

    local function getServerFighter(character)
        return character and character:FindFirstChild("ServerFighter")
            or (character and character:GetAttribute("server_fighter"))
    end

    local function getTargetPart(character)
        if Toggles.aimbot_closest_part.Value then
            local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
            for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                    local dist = (part.Position - cam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                    if dist < bestDist then
                        bestDist = dist
                        best = part
                    end
                end
            end
            return best
        end
        local preferred = Options.targeting_part.Value
        if preferred then
            local part = character:FindFirstChild(preferred)
            if part then return part end
        end
        return character:FindFirstChild("HitboxHead")
            or character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    local function acquire()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return nil end

        local radius = Options.aimbot_radius.Value
        local origin = cam.CFrame.Position
        local best, bestScore = nil, math.huge

        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player == LocalPlayer then continue end
            local character = player.Character
            if not character then continue end

            local fighter = getClientFighter(character)
            if not fighter then continue end
            local server = getServerFighter(character)
            local data = fighter.Data or (server and server.Data)

            local hum = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then continue end
            if isAlly(character) then continue end
            if isFlashbanged(character) and Toggles.targeting_disableflash.Value then continue end

            local crouching = hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Crouching
            if crouching and Toggles.targeting_disableflash.Value then
                continue
            end

            local part = getTargetPart(character)
            if not part then continue end

            local screen, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
            if not onScreen then continue end

            local distance = (part.Position - origin).Magnitude
            if distance > radius then continue end

            local viewport = cam.ViewportSize
            local center = Vector2.new(viewport.X / 2, viewport.Y / 2)
            local score = (Vector2.new(screen.X, screen.Y) - center).Magnitude
            if score < bestScore then
                bestScore = score
                best = {
                    player = player,
                    character = character,
                    fighter = fighter,
                    server = server,
                    data = data,
                    part = part,
                    position = part.Position,
                    distance = distance,
                    manipulated = data and data.manipulated or false,
                }
            end
        end
        self.target = best
        return best
    end

    function self:isManipulated(target)
        return target and target.manipulated or self.manipulated
    end

    local function updateFov()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local viewport = cam.ViewportSize
        local radius = Options.aimbot_radius.Value
        local lerp = Options.aimbot_fov_lerp.Value

        local focal = (viewport.X / 2) / math.tan(math.rad(cam.FieldOfView) / 2)
        local pixelRadius = focal * math.tan(math.rad(radius))

        local center = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(cam.CFrame.Position)

        fovCircle.Radius = fovCircle.Radius + (pixelRadius - fovCircle.Radius) * lerp
        fovCircle.Position = Vector2.new(center.X, center.Y)
        fovCircle.Color = Options.aimbot_color1.Value

        fovFill.Radius = fovCircle.Radius
        fovFill.Position = fovCircle.Position
        fovFill.Color = Options.aimbot_fill_color1.Value

        local rotationSpeed = Options.aimbot_fov_rotation_speed.Value
        local rotation = Options.aimbot_fov_rotation.Value
        if Toggles.aimbot_fov_moving.Value then
            rotation = rotation + rotationSpeed * tick()
        end
    end

    local function onRender()
        local toggle = Toggles.aimbot_toggle.Value
        local showFov = Toggles.aimbot_showfov.Value

        if not toggle then
            fovCircle.Visible = false
            fovFill.Visible = false
            return
        end

        updateFov()

        local fovSettings = Options.aimbot_fov_settings.Value or {}
        fovCircle.Visible = showFov and fovSettings["position on target"]
        fovFill.Visible = showFov and Toggles.aimbot_fov_fill.Value

        local target = acquire()
        if not target then return end

        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local look = cam.CFrame.LookVector
        local toTarget = (target.position - cam.CFrame.Position).Unit
        local dot = look:Dot(toTarget)
        if dot < math.cos(math.rad(Options.aimbot_radius.Value)) then
            return
        end

        local matchAxis = Options.aimbot_match_axis.Value
        local smoothing = Options.aimbot_smoothing.Value

        if matchAxis == "y" then
            local yaw = math.atan2(
                target.position.X - cam.CFrame.Position.X,
                target.position.Z - cam.CFrame.Position.Z
            )
            local pitch = math.asin(math.clamp(
                (target.position.Y - cam.CFrame.Position.Y)
                    / (target.position - cam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude, -1, 1
            ))
            local currentYaw = math.atan2(cam.CFrame.LookVector.X, cam.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
            local delta = (yaw - currentYaw + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi) - math.pi
            cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position)
                * CFrame.Angles(0, delta * smoothing, 0)
                * CFrame.Angles(-pitch * smoothing, 0, 0)
        else
            cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.lookAt(cam.CFrame.Position, target.position), smoothing)
        end
    end

    function self:getTarget()
        if not Toggles.aimbot_toggle.Value then return nil end
        if not self:keyHeld() then return nil end
        return acquire()
    end

    function self:keyHeld()
        local mode = Toggles.aimbot_keybind.Mode
        local key = Toggles.aimbot_keybind.Value
        if mode == "Always" then
            return true
        end
        if mode == "Hold" then
            return UserInputService:IsKeyDown(key)
        end
        return true
    end

    function self:destroy()
        pcall(function()
            fovCircle:Remove()
        end)
        pcall(function()
            fovFill:Remove()
        end)
    end

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(onRender)

    return self
end

-- ============================================================================

-- MOVEMENT

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local Movement = {}
Movement.__index = Movement

function Movement.new(characters)
    local self = setmetatable({}, Movement)
    self.characters = characters

    self.humanoid = nil
    self.humanoidRootPart = nil
    self.currentVehicle = nil
    self.events = {
        force_cframe_change = Instance.new("BindableEvent"),
    }

    local function isTyping()
        local textbox = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
        return textbox ~= nil
    end

    local function getHumanoid()
        return LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    end

    local function getRootPart()
        return LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    local function flyLoop(step)
        if not Toggles.movement_fly.Value then return end
        if not self:keyHeld() then return end
        if isTyping() then return end
        if self.currentVehicle then return end

        local hum = getHumanoid()
        if not hum then return end
        local root = getRootPart()
        if not root then return end

        hum.PlatformStand = true

        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local cf = root.CFrame

        local move = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then move = move + cam.CFrame.LookVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then move = move - cam.CFrame.LookVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then move = move - cam.CFrame.RightVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then move = move + cam.CFrame.RightVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then move = move + Vector3.yAxis end

        local speed = Options.movement_flyvalue.Value
        if move.Magnitude > 0 then
            move = move.Unit * speed
        end

        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = cf:VectorToWorldSpace(move)
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

        local yaw = select(2, cam.CFrame:ToOrientation())
        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0)
    end

    local function noclipLoop()
        if not Toggles.movement_noclip.Value then return end
        if isTyping() then return end
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not character then return end
        for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.CanCollide = false
                part.CanTouch = false
                part.CanQuery = false
            end
        end
    end

    local function velocityLoop()
        if not Toggles.movement_velocity.Value then return end
        local root = getRootPart()
        if not root then return end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local speed = Options.movement_velocityvalue.Value
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = cam.CFrame.LookVector * speed
    end

    local function slideBoostLoop()
        if not Toggles.movement_slideboost.Value then return end
        local hum = getHumanoid()
        if not hum then return end
        if hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Sliding then
            local root = getRootPart()
            if root then
                local boost = Options.movement_slideboostvalue.Value
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity * boost
            end
        end
    end

    local lastJump = 0
    local jumpsUsed = 0

    local function doubleJumpLoop()
        local hum = getHumanoid()
        if not hum then return end
        local root = getRootPart()
        if not root then return end

        local grounded = hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running
            or hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Idle

        if grounded then
            jumpsUsed = 0
            return
        end

        local jumpPressed = UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space)
        local limit = Toggles.movement_infinite_double_jump.Value and 999 or 1

        if jumpPressed and jumpsUsed < limit then
            local now = tick()
            if now - lastJump > 0.15 then
                lastJump = now
                jumpsUsed = jumpsUsed + 1
                local height = Options.movement_double_jump_height_multiplier.Value or 1
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X,
                    hum.JumpPower * height,
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z
                )
            end
        end
    end

    local function cleanup()
        self.humanoid = nil
        self.humanoidRootPart = nil
        self.currentVehicle = nil
    end

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(flyLoop)
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(velocityLoop)
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(slideBoostLoop)
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(doubleJumpLoop)
    RunService.Stepped:Connect(noclipLoop)

    if self.characters and self.characters.CharacterRemoving then
        self.characters.CharacterRemoving:Connect(cleanup)
    end

    return self
end

function Movement:keyHeld()
    local mode = Toggles.movement_flykeybind.Mode
    local key = Toggles.movement_flykeybind.Value
    if mode == "Always" then
        return true
    end
    if mode == "Hold" then
        return UserInputService:IsKeyDown(key)
    end
    if mode == "Toggle" then
        return Toggles.movement_flykeybind.KeyDown
    end
    return true
end

function Movement.setCharacter(self, character)
    self.character = character
    self.humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
    self.humanoidRootPart = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

-- ============================================================================

-- HIT EFFECTS & SOUNDS

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local InsertService = game:GetService("InsertService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local Effects = {}
Effects.__index = Effects

local EFFECT_TYPES = { "fortnite damage", "hit marker", "phantom forces" }

function Effects.new()
    local self = setmetatable({}, Effects)
    self.effects = {}
    self.markers = {}
    self.particles = {}
    self.colorIndex = 0

    self.colors = {
        Options.hit_effects_col_1.Value,
        Options.hit_effects_col_2.Value,
        Options.hit_effects_col_3.Value,
    }

    Options.hit_effects_selected:SetValues(EFFECT_TYPES)

    local function cycleColor()
        self.colorIndex = self.colorIndex + 1
        return self.colors[(self.colorIndex % #self.colors) + 1]
    end

    local function getType()
        return Options.hit_effects_selected.Value or "fortnite damage"
    end

    self.stackWindows = {}
    local STACK_WINDOW = 0.15

    local function stackDamage(character, damage)
        local now = tick()
        local existing = self.stackWindows[character]
        if existing and now - existing.time < STACK_WINDOW then
            existing.damage = existing.damage + damage
            existing.time = now
            local label = existing.label
            if label then
                label.Text = tostring(math.round(existing.damage))
            end
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

    function self:spawnDamageMarker(character, damage, hitPart)
        if Toggles.hit_effects_disable_marker.Value then return end
        if not character then return end

        if stackDamage(character, damage) then
            return
        end

        local color = cycleColor()
        local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head") or hitPart or character

        local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        gui.Name = "BillboardDamage"
        gui.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
        gui.ClipsDescendants = false
        gui.LightInfluence = 0
        gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        gui.Adornee = head
        gui.Parent = character

        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.Name = "TextDamage"
        label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Text = tostring(math.round(damage))
        label.TextColor3 = color
        label.TextSize = 60
        label.Font = Enum.Font.Zekton
        label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
        label.Parent = gui

        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        stroke.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        stroke.Thickness = 4
        stroke.Parent = label

        local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
        scale.Scale = 1
        scale.Parent = label

        local shadow = label:Clone()
        shadow.Name = "TextDamageShadow"
        shadow.TextColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        shadow.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
        shadow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3)
        shadow.Parent = gui

        local tween = TweenService:Create(gui, TweenInfo.new(
            1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out
        ), { Position = gui.Position + UDim2.fromOffset(0, -50) })
        tween:Play()

        self.stackWindows[character] = {
            time = tick(),
            damage = damage,
            label = label,
        }
        task.delay(1.2, function()
            if self.stackWindows[character] and self.stackWindows[character].label == label then
                self.stackWindows[character] = nil
            end
            gui:Destroy()
        end)

        table.insert(self.markers, gui)
    end

    function self:playShootSound(weapon)
        if not Toggles.shoot_sound_change.Value then return end
        if not LocalPlayer.Character then return end
        local volume = Options.shoot_sound_volume.Value
        local speed = Options.shoot_sound_speed.Value
        local custom = Options.shoot_sound_custom_value.Value
        local sound = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("ShootSound_" .. weapon)
        if not sound then
            sound = Instance.new("Sound")
            sound.Name = "ShootSound_" .. weapon
            sound.SoundId = custom ~= "" and "rbxassetid://" .. custom or "rbxassetid://5617972474"
            sound.Volume = volume
            sound.PlaybackSpeed = speed
            sound.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
        end
        sound.Volume = volume
        sound.PlaybackSpeed = speed
        sound:Play()
    end

    function self:spawnHitEffect(character, hitPart)
        if not Toggles.hit_effects_enabled.Value then return end
        if Toggles.hit_effects_disable_numbers.Value then return end
        if not hitPart then return end

        local effectType = getType()
        if effectType == "phantom forces" then
            self:spawnPhantomClone(character, hitPart)
        elseif effectType == "hit marker" then
            self:spawnHitMarker(character, hitPart)
        else
            self:spawnFortniteClone(character, hitPart)
        end
    end

    function self:spawnFortniteClone(character, hitPart)
        local clone = hitPart:Clone()
        clone.Parent = workspace
        clone.CFrame = hitPart.CFrame
        clone.CanCollide = false
        clone.CanQuery = false
        clone.CanTouch = false
        clone.Anchored = true
        clone.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic

        local material = Options.hit_effects_material.Value
        if material and material ~= "Default" then
            pcall(function()
                clone.Material = Enum.Material[material]
            end)
        end

        if Toggles.hit_effects_weld.Value then
            local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
            weld.Part0 = hitPart
            weld.Part1 = clone
            weld.Parent = clone
        end

        for _, child in ipairs(clone:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Accessory") or child:IsA("Motor6D") or child:IsA("Weld") then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end

        table.insert(self.effects, clone)

        local tween = TweenService:Create(clone, TweenInfo.new(
            0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out
        ), { Transparency = 1 })
        tween:Play()

        task.delay(0.5, function()
            clone:Destroy()
        end)
    end

    function self:spawnPhantomClone(character, hitPart)
        local clone = hitPart:Clone()
        clone.Parent = workspace
        clone.CFrame = hitPart.CFrame
        clone.CanCollide = false
        clone.CanQuery = false
        clone.CanTouch = false
        clone.Anchored = true
        clone.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        clone.Transparency = 0.5

        if Toggles.hit_effects_weld.Value then
            local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
            weld.Part0 = hitPart
            weld.Part1 = clone
            weld.Parent = clone
        end

        for _, child in ipairs(clone:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Accessory") or child:IsA("Motor6D") or child:IsA("Weld") then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end

        table.insert(self.effects, clone)

        local tween = TweenService:Create(clone, TweenInfo.new(
            0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out
        ), { Transparency = 1 })
        tween:Play()

        task.delay(0.4, function()
            clone:Destroy()
        end)
    end

    function self:spawnHitMarker(character, hitPart)
        local head = character and (character:FindFirstChild("Head") or hitPart)
        if not head then return end

        local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        gui.Name = "HitMarker"
        gui.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
        gui.ClipsDescendants = false
        gui.Adornee = head
        gui.Parent = character

        local cross = Instance.new("Frame")
        cross.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        cross.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        cross.Parent = gui

        local color = cycleColor()
        local thickness = UDim2.fromScale(0.02, 0.25)

        local vert = Instance.new("Frame")
        vert.Size = thickness
        vert.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        vert.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
        vert.BackgroundColor3 = color
        vert.Parent = cross

        local horiz = Instance.new("Frame")
        horiz.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.25, 0.02)
        horiz.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        horiz.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
        horiz.BackgroundColor3 = color
        horiz.Parent = cross

        local tween = TweenService:Create(gui, TweenInfo.new(
            0.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out
        ), { Size = gui.Size * 1.5 })
        tween:Play()

        task.delay(0.6, function()
            gui:Destroy()
        end)
    end

    function self:spawnExplosion(position, radius)
        local explosion = Instance.new("Explosion")
        explosion.BlastRadius = radius or 4
        explosion.Position = position
        explosion.DestroyJointRadiusPercent = 0
        explosion.Parent = workspace
    end

    function self:spawnParticleBurst(character, position)
        local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
        attachment.WorldPosition = position
        attachment.Parent = character

        local emitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
        emitter.Rate = 0
        emitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.5)
        emitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(5, 10)
        emitter.VelocityPerpendicular = NumberRange.new(0, 4)
        emitter.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-90, 90)
        emitter.Color = ColorSequence.new(cycleColor())
        emitter.Size = NumberSequence.new(1)
        emitter.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
        emitter.Parent = attachment

        emitter:Emit(15)
        task.delay(1.5, function()
            attachment:Destroy()
        end)
    end

    function self:playHitSound()
        if not Toggles.hit_sounds_enabled.Value then return end
        if Toggles.hit_sounds_disable.Value then return end
        if not LocalPlayer.Character then return end

        local selected = Options.hit_sounds_selected.Value or "sparkle"
        local volume = Options.hit_sounds_volume.Value

        local sound = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HitSound_" .. selected)
        if not sound then
            sound = Instance.new("Sound")
            sound.Name = "HitSound_" .. selected
            sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. HIT_SOUND_ASSETS[selected]
            sound.Volume = volume
            sound.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
        end
        sound.Volume = volume
        sound:Play()
    end

    function self:registerHit(targetCharacter, damage, hitPart, hitPosition)
        self:spawnDamageMarker(targetCharacter, damage, hitPart)
        self:spawnHitEffect(targetCharacter, hitPart)
        if hitPosition then
            self:spawnParticleBurst(targetCharacter or workspace, hitPosition)
        end
        self:playHitSound()
    end

    Options.hit_effects_col_1.OnChanged:Connect(function(value)
        self.colors[1] = value
    end)
    Options.hit_effects_col_2.OnChanged:Connect(function(value)
        self.colors[2] = value
    end)
    Options.hit_effects_col_3.OnChanged:Connect(function(value)
        self.colors[3] = value
    end)

    return self
end

local HIT_SOUND_ASSETS = {
    sparkle = "5070376932",
    ding = "5617972474",
    thud = "4539867272",
    ping = "5070394352",
}

function Effects:destroy()
    for _, effect in ipairs(self.effects) do
        pcall(function()
            effect:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    for _, marker in ipairs(self.markers) do
        pcall(function()
            marker:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    self.effects = {}
    self.markers = {}
    self.particles = {}
end

-- ============================================================================

-- ESP

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local ESP = {}
ESP.__index = ESP

local SKELETON_PARTS = {
    "RightHand", "LeftLowerArm", "RightLowerArm", "LeftUpperArm", "RightUpperArm",
    "LowerTorso", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftFoot", "RightFoot",
    "RightLowerLeg", "RightUpperLeg", "UpperTorso", "LeftHand", "Head",
}

local SKELETON_LINES = {
    { "LeftUpperArm", "UpperTorso" },
    { "LeftLowerArm", "LeftUpperArm" },
    { "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand" },
    { "RightUpperArm", "UpperTorso" },
    { "RightLowerArm", "RightUpperArm" },
    { "RightHand", "RightLowerArm" },
    { "LeftUpperLeg", "LowerTorso" },
    { "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftUpperLeg" },
    { "LeftFoot", "LeftLowerLeg" },
    { "RightUpperLeg", "LowerTorso" },
    { "RightLowerLeg", "RightUpperLeg" },
    { "RightFoot", "RightLowerLeg" },
    { "LowerTorso", "UpperTorso" },
}

local SMALL_FONTS = {
    ["smallest pixel"] = 8,
    ["proggy tiny"] = 10,
    ["pixel arial"] = 11,
}

function ESP.new()
    local self = setmetatable({}, ESP)
    self.players = {}
    self.events = {
        esp_color_changed = Instance.new("BindableEvent"),
        esp_fill_changed = Instance.new("BindableEvent"),
        esp_effects_changed = Instance.new("BindableEvent"),
        esp_effects_color_changed = Instance.new("BindableEvent"),
    }

    local function updatePlayerState(player, character)
        local state = self.players[player]
        if not state then
            state = self:createState(player, character)
            self.players[player] = state
        end
        state.character = character
        state.humanoidRootPart = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
        state.humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
        state.parts = {}

        local connections = state.connections
        table.insert(connections, state.humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
            state.health = state.humanoid.Health
        end))
        table.insert(connections, state.humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("MaxHealth"):Connect(function()
            state.maxHealth = state.humanoid.MaxHealth
        end))
        state.health = state.humanoid.Health
        state.maxHealth = state.humanoid.MaxHealth

        for _, partName in ipairs(SKELETON_PARTS) do
            local part = character:WaitForChild(partName, 10)
            if not part then continue end
            state.parts[partName] = part
            state.corners[partName] = self:computeCorners(part)
        end

        task.wait()
    end

    return self
end

function ESP:computeCorners(part)
    local size = part.Size / 2
    local corners = {}
    local x, y, z = size.X, size.Y, size.Z
    table.insert(corners, Vector3.new(-x, -y, -z))
    table.insert(corners, Vector3.new(-x, -y, z))
    table.insert(corners, Vector3.new(-x, y, -z))
    table.insert(corners, Vector3.new(-x, y, z))
    table.insert(corners, Vector3.new(x, -y, -z))
    table.insert(corners, Vector3.new(x, -y, z))
    table.insert(corners, Vector3.new(x, y, -z))
    table.insert(corners, Vector3.new(x, y, z))
    return corners
end

local IMAGES = {
    default = { image = "", size = 1 },
    circle = { image = "rbxassetid://133898707", size = 1 },
    diamond = { image = "rbxassetid://146699083", size = 1 },
    heart = { image = "rbxassetid://134603269", size = 1 },
}

function ESP:createState(player, character)
    local state = {
        player = player,
        character = character,
        humanoid = nil,
        humanoidRootPart = nil,
        health = 0,
        maxHealth = 150,
        parts = {},
        corners = {},
        connections = {},
        drawings = {},
        squares = {},
        images = {},
        lines = {},
        text = {},
    }

    for i = 1, 26 do
        local outline = i > 13
        local line = Drawing.new("Line")
        line.Visible = false
        line.Thickness = 1 + (outline and 2 or 0)
        line.Color = outline and Color3.new(0, 0, 0) or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        table.insert(state.drawings, line)
        table.insert(state.lines, line)
    end

    for i = 1, 4 do
        local text = Drawing.new("Text")
        text.Outline = true
        text.Visible = false
        text.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        text.Center = true
        text.Middle = true
        text.Size = 12
        table.insert(state.drawings, text)
        table.insert(state.text, text)
    end

    for i = 1, 4 do
        local square = Drawing.new("Square")
        square.Visible = false
        square.Outline = true
        table.insert(state.drawings, square)
        table.insert(state.squares, square)
    end

    local image = Drawing.new("Image")
    image.Visible = false
    image.Outline = true
    image.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    image.Filled = true
    table.insert(state.drawings, image)
    table.insert(state.images, image)

    state.squares[1].Thickness = 3
    state.squares[2].ZIndex = 2
    state.squares[2].Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    state.squares[3].ZIndex = 2
    state.squares[3].Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    state.squares[3].Filled = true
    state.squares[4].Filled = true
    state.squares[4].Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)

    state.text[1].Text = state.player.Name
    state.text[2].Text = "none"
    state.text[4].RichText = true
    state.text[4].Center = false

    local fillImage = Options.esp_fill_image.Value
    local fillConfig = IMAGES[fillImage] or IMAGES.default
    state.images[1].Image = fillConfig.image
    state.images[1].Center = true
    state.fillScale = fillConfig.size

    return state
end

function ESP:computeBounds(state)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local dynamic = Options.esp_bounds_type.Value == "dynamic"
    local widthScale = Options.esp_bounds_width_scale.Value / 100

    local function worldToScreen(position)
        local screen, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(position)
        return Vector2.new(screen.X, screen.Y), onScreen
    end

    if dynamic then
        local minX, minY = math.huge, math.huge
        local maxX, maxY = -math.huge, -math.huge
        local valid = false
        for partName, part in pairs(state.parts) do
            local corners = state.corners[partName]
            if not corners then continue end
            for _, offset in ipairs(corners) do
                local screen, onScreen = worldToScreen((part.CFrame * offset).Position)
                if onScreen then
                    valid = true
                    minX = math.min(minX, screen.X)
                    maxX = math.max(maxX, screen.X)
                    minY = math.min(minY, screen.Y)
                    maxY = math.max(maxY, screen.Y)
                end
            end
        end
        if not valid then return nil end
        local width = math.max(10, maxX - minX)
        local height = math.max(10, maxY - minY)
        return width * widthScale, height * widthScale, Vector2.new(minX + (maxX - minX) / 2, minY)
    end

    local hrp = state.humanoidRootPart
    if not hrp then return nil end
    local top, topOn = worldToScreen((hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2.5, 0)).Position)
    local bottom, bottomOn = worldToScreen((hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -3.5, 0)).Position)
    if not topOn or not bottomOn then return nil end
    local height = math.max(10, (top - bottom).Magnitude)
    return height * 0.55 * widthScale * 2, height * widthScale, bottom
end

function ESP:drawSkeleton(state)
    local visible = Toggles.esp_skeleton.Value
    for i, line in ipairs(state.lines) do
        line.Visible = false
    end
    if not visible then return end
    if not state.character then return end

    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local outline = Toggles.esp_skeleton_outline.Value

    for i, pair in ipairs(SKELETON_LINES) do
        local fromPart = state.parts[pair[1]]
        local toPart = state.parts[pair[2]]
        if not fromPart or not toPart then continue end

        local from, onFrom = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(fromPart.Position)
        local to, onTo = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(toPart.Position)
        if not onFrom or not onTo then continue end

        local line = state.lines[i]
        local outlineLine = state.lines[i + 13]

        line.From = Vector2.new(from.X, from.Y)
        line.To = Vector2.new(to.X, to.Y)
        line.Visible = true

        if outline then
            outlineLine.From = line.From
            outlineLine.To = line.To
            outlineLine.Visible = true
        end
    end
end

function ESP:drawBox(state, size, position)
    local box = Toggles.esp_box.Value
    local fill = Toggles.esp_fill.Value
    local fillImage = Options.esp_fill_image.Value

    state.squares[1].Visible = false
    state.squares[2].Visible = false
    state.squares[3].Visible = false
    state.squares[4].Visible = false
    state.images[1].Visible = false

    if not box and not fill then return end

    local half = Vector2.new(size.X / 2, 0)

    if box then
        state.squares[1].Size = size
        state.squares[1].Position = position - half
        state.squares[1].Visible = true

        state.squares[2].Size = size
        state.squares[2].Position = position - half
        state.squares[2].Visible = true
    end

    if fill then
        if fillImage ~= "" then
            state.images[1].Size = size
            state.images[1].Position = position - half
            state.images[1].Visible = true
        else
            state.squares[3].Size = size
            state.squares[3].Position = position - half
            state.squares[3].Visible = true

            state.squares[4].Size = size
            state.squares[4].Position = position - half
            state.squares[4].Visible = true
        end
    end
end

function ESP:drawName(state, position)
    local name = state.text[1]
    name.Visible = false
    if not Toggles.esp_name.Value then return end

    name.Text = state.player.Name
    name.Position = position - Vector2.new(0, 2)
    name.Visible = true
end

function ESP:drawWeapon(state, position)
    local weapon = state.text[2]
    weapon.Visible = false
    if not Toggles.esp_weapon.Value then return end

    local character = state.character
    local item = character and character:FindFirstChild("EquippedItem")
    weapon.Text = item and item.Name or "none"
    weapon.Position = position + Vector2.new(0, weapon.TextBounds.Y + 1)
    weapon.Visible = true
end

function ESP:drawDistance(state, position)
    local distance = state.text[3]
    distance.Visible = false
    if not Toggles.esp_distance.Value then return end

    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local origin = cam and cam.CFrame.Position or Vector3.new()
    local dist = (state.humanoidRootPart.Position - origin).Magnitude
    distance.Text = "[" .. math.round(dist) .. "]"
    distance.Position = position + Vector2.new(0, state.text[2].Visible and (distance.TextBounds.Y + 8) or distance.TextBounds.Y + 2)
    distance.Visible = true
end

function ESP:drawHealthbar(state, size, position)
    state.squares[3].Visible = false
    state.squares[4].Visible = false
    if not Toggles.esp_healthbar.Value then return end
    if not state.humanoid then return end

    local health = state.humanoid.Health
    local maxHealth = state.humanoid.MaxHealth
    local ratio = math.clamp(health / maxHealth, 0, 1)

    local lerpSpeed = Options.esp_healthbar_health_lerp.Value
    state.lerpedHealth = state.lerpedHealth or ratio
    state.lerpedHealth = state.lerpedHealth + (ratio - state.lerpedHealth) * lerpSpeed

    local barSize = Vector2.new(4, size.Y - 2)
    if Toggles.esp_healthbar_resize.Value then
        barSize = Vector2.new(4, (size.Y - 2) * state.lerpedHealth)
    end

    local barPosition = position - Vector2.new(size.X / 2 + 6, -size.Y + 1) - Vector2.new(-1, 2)
    local bgPosition = barPosition - Vector2.new(1, -1)

    state.squares[4].Size = Vector2.new(4, size.Y)
    state.squares[4].Position = bgPosition
    state.squares[4].Visible = true

    state.squares[3].Size = barSize
    state.squares[3].Position = barPosition
    state.squares[3].Visible = true
end

function ESP:drawFlags(state, position)
    local flags = state.text[4]
    flags.Visible = false
    if not Toggles.esp_flag_health_text.Value and not Toggles.esp_flag_staring_text.Value then return end

    local text = ""
    local prefix = Options.esp_flag_prefix.Value or "minimal"
    local prefixMap = { ["bridge only"] = "", minimal = "HP", full = "HEALTH" }
    local prefixText = prefixMap[prefix] or ""

    if Toggles.esp_flag_health_text.Value and state.humanoid then
        text = text .. "[" .. prefixText .. " " .. math.round(state.humanoid.Health) .. "]"
    end

    if Toggles.esp_flag_staring_text.Value then
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local origin = cam and cam.CFrame.Position or Vector3.new()
        local dist = (state.humanoidRootPart.Position - origin).Magnitude
        if dist < 25 then
            text = text .. "staring"
        end
    end

    flags.Text = text
    flags.Position = position + Vector2.new(state.text[1].TextBounds.Y / 2 + 2, 0)
    flags.Visible = text ~= ""
end

function ESP:drawEffects(state)
    if not state.character then return end
    local ally = state.character:FindFirstChild("_is_ally")
    if ally and ally.Value then return end

    if Toggles.esp_effects_aura.Value then
        local aura = state.character:FindFirstChild("ESP_Aura")
        if not aura then
            local newAura = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
            newAura.Name = "ESP_Aura"
            newAura.Adornee = state.character
            newAura.Color3 = Options.esp_effects_aura_color1.Value
            newAura.LineThickness = 1
            newAura.Transparency = 1
            newAura.SurfaceTransparency = 1
            newAura.Parent = state.character
        end
    else
        local aura = state.character:FindFirstChild("ESP_Aura")
        if aura then aura:Destroy() end
    end

    if Toggles.esp_effects_particle.Value then
        local particleFolder = state.character:FindFirstChild("ESP_Particles")
        if not particleFolder then
            particleFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
            particleFolder.Name = "ESP_Particles"
            particleFolder.Parent = state.character
        end
        if #particleFolder:GetChildren() == 0 then
            if state.humanoidRootPart then
                local emitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
                emitter.Color = ColorSequence.new(Options.esp_effects_particle_color1.Value)
                emitter.Size = NumberSequence.new(0.5)
                emitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.5)
                emitter.Rate = 10
                emitter.Parent = state.humanoidRootPart
            end
        end
    else
        local particleFolder = state.character:FindFirstChild("ESP_Particles")
        if particleFolder then particleFolder:Destroy() end
    end
end

function ESP:drawWorldElements(state)
    local character = state.character
    if not character then return end
    local hrp = state.humanoidRootPart
    if not hrp then return end

    local whitelist = Options.esp_world_whitelist.Value or {}
    local showName = Toggles.esp_world_name.Value
    local showDistance = Toggles.esp_world_distance.Value
    local showImage = Toggles.esp_world_image.Value

    local existing = character:FindFirstChild("ESP_WorldName")
    if showName then
        if not existing then
            local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            billboard.Name = "ESP_WorldName"
            billboard.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
            billboard.Adornee = hrp
            billboard.MaxDistance = Options.esp_world_distance.Value * 10

            local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            label.Text = character.Name
            label.TextColor3 = Options.esp_world_name_color.Value
            label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            label.Parent = billboard

            billboard.Parent = character
        end
    elseif existing then
        existing:Destroy()
    end

    local distanceBillboard = character:FindFirstChild("ESP_WorldDistance")
    if showDistance then
        if not distanceBillboard then
            local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            billboard.Name = "ESP_WorldDistance"
            billboard.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
            billboard.Adornee = hrp
            billboard.MaxDistance = Options.esp_world_distance.Value * 10

            local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            label.Text = "0"
            label.TextColor3 = Options.esp_world_distance_color.Value
            label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            label.Parent = billboard

            billboard.Parent = character
        else
            local label = distanceBillboard:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
            if label then
                local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                local dist = (hrp.Position - cam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                label.Text = "[" .. math.round(dist) .. "]"
            end
        end
    elseif distanceBillboard then
        distanceBillboard:Destroy()
    end

    local imageBillboard = character:FindFirstChild("ESP_WorldImage")
    if showImage then
        if not imageBillboard then
            local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            billboard.Name = "ESP_WorldImage"
            billboard.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
            billboard.Adornee = hrp
            billboard.MaxDistance = Options.esp_world_distance.Value * 10

            local image = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            image.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            image.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            image.Image = Options.esp_world_image.Value
            image.Parent = billboard

            billboard.Parent = character
        end
    elseif imageBillboard then
        imageBillboard:Destroy()
    end
end

function ESP:hideAll(state)
    for _, drawing in ipairs(state.drawings) do
        drawing.Visible = false
    end
end

function ESP:drawChams(state)
    if not state.character then return end
    local chams = Toggles.esp_chams.Value
    local override = Toggles.esp_override_appearance.Value

    local highlight = state.character:FindFirstChild("ESP_Chams")
    if not chams then
        if highlight then highlight:Destroy() end
        return
    end

    if not highlight then
        highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
        highlight.Name = "ESP_Chams"
        highlight.FillColor = Options.esp_chams_color.Value
        highlight.OutlineColor = Options.esp_chams_color2.Value or Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        highlight.FillTransparency = Options.esp_chams_alpha_fill.Value
        highlight.OutlineTransparency = Options.esp_chams_alpha_outline.Value
        highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        highlight.Parent = state.character
    end

    if override then
        highlight.FillColor = Options.esp_chams_color.Value
    end
end

function ESP:drawBodyAppearance(state)
    if not state.character then return end
    if not Toggles.esp_body_color.Value then return end
    local transparency = Options.esp_body_transparency.Value or 0
    for _, part in ipairs(state.character:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Transparency < 1 then
            part.Transparency = transparency
            part.Color = Options.esp_body_color_value.Value
        end
    end
end

function ESP:update(player)
    local state = self.players[player]
    if not state then return end
    if not Toggles.esp_name.Value and not Toggles.esp_distance.Value
        and not Toggles.esp_weapon.Value and not Toggles.esp_box.Value
        and not Toggles.esp_fill.Value and not Toggles.esp_skeleton.Value
        and not Toggles.esp_healthbar.Value and not Toggles.esp_flag_health_text.Value
        and not Toggles.esp_flag_staring_text.Value then
        self:hideAll(state)
        return
    end

    local character = state.character
    if not character then
        self:hideAll(state)
        return
    end

    local ally = character:FindFirstChild("_is_ally")
    if not Toggles.esp_show_team.Value and ally and ally.Value then
        self:hideAll(state)
        return
    end

    if state.health <= 0 then
        self:hideAll(state)
        return
    end

    local bounds = self:computeBounds(state)
    if not bounds then
        self:hideAll(state)
        return
    end

    local size, height, position = bounds
    local sizeVec = Vector2.new(size, height)

    self:drawSkeleton(state)
    self:drawChams(state)
    self:drawBodyAppearance(state)
    self:drawBox(state, sizeVec, position)
    self:drawName(state, position)
    self:drawWeapon(state, position)
    self:drawDistance(state, position)
    self:drawHealthbar(state, sizeVec, position)
    self:drawFlags(state, position)
end

function ESP:start()
    local self = self

    local function onPlayerAdded(player)
        local state = self:createState(player, nil)
        self.players[player] = state

        local function onCharacterAdded(character)
            self:updatePlayerState(player, character)
        end

        table.insert(state.connections, player.CharacterAdded:Connect(onCharacterAdded))
        table.insert(state.connections, player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
            self:hideAll(state)
            state.character = nil
        end))

        if player.Character then
            task.spawn(function()
                onCharacterAdded(player.Character)
            end)
        end
    end

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        onPlayerAdded(player)
    end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(onPlayerAdded)

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        for player in pairs(self.players) do
            self:update(player)
        end
    end)

    self.events.esp_color_changed.Event:Connect(function()
        for _, state in pairs(self.players) do
            if state.character then
                self:drawEffects(state)
            end
        end
    end)

    self.events.esp_effects_changed.Event:Connect(function()
        for _, state in pairs(self.players) do
            if state.character then
                self:drawEffects(state)
            end
        end
    end)

    self.events.esp_effects_color_changed.Event:Connect(function()
        for _, state in pairs(self.players) do
            if state.character then
                self:drawEffects(state)
            end
        end
    end)

    return self
end

function ESP:destroy()
    for player, state in pairs(self.players) do
        for _, connection in ipairs(state.connections) do
            pcall(function()
                connection:Disconnect()
            end)
        end
        for _, drawing in ipairs(state.drawings) do
            pcall(function()
                drawing:Remove()
            end)
        end
        self.players[player] = nil
    end
end

-- ============================================================================

-- WORLD - CROSSHAIR / HIGHLIGHT / TRACER / WEATHER / SKYBOX

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local World = {}
World.__index = World

local function makeLine()
    local line = Drawing.new("Line")
    line.Thickness = 1
    line.Transparency = 1
    line.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    line.Visible = false
    return line
end

function World.new()
    local self = setmetatable({}, World)
    self.drawings = {}
    self.tracerLines = {}
    self.players = {}

    self.tracerLine = makeLine()
    table.insert(self.drawings, self.tracerLine)

    local function crosshairRender()
        if not Toggles.crosshair_enabled.Value then return end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local center = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(cam.CFrame.Position)

        local settings = Options.crosshair_settings.Value or {}
        local length = Options.crosshair_length.Value
        local thickness = Options.crosshair_thickness.Value
        local offset = Options.crosshair_offset.Value
        local lerp = Options.crosshair_lerp.Value
        local rotation = Options.crosshair_rotation.Value
        local rotationSpeed = Options.crosshair_rotation_speed.Value
        local outline = Toggles.crosshair_outline.Value

        local color = Options.crosshair_color_3.Value or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        local outlineColor = Options.crosshair_outline_color_1.Value or Color3.new(0, 0, 0)

        if Toggles.crosshair_rotation.Value and Options.crosshair_rotation_speed.Value > 0 then
            rotation = rotation + rotationSpeed * tick()
        end

        local cx, cy = center.X, center.Y
        local arm = length / 2 + offset
        local gap = offset

        local function drawArm(dx, dy)
            local from = Vector2.new(cx, cy)
            local to = Vector2.new(cx + dx * (gap + arm), cy + dy * (gap + arm))
            local from2 = Vector2.new(cx + dx * gap, cy + dy * gap)
            local from3 = Vector2.new(cx + dx * (gap + arm * 0.9), cy + dy * (gap + arm * 0.9))

            local line = self.tracerLine
            line.From = from2
            line.To = to
            line.Thickness = thickness
            line.Color = color
            line.Transparency = 0
            line.Visible = true
        end

        local lines = {}
        for i = 1, 4 do
            local line = Drawing.new("Line")
            line.Thickness = thickness
            line.Color = color
            line.Visible = false
            table.insert(self.drawings, line)
            table.insert(lines, line)
        end

        local dirs = { {1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1} }
        for i, d in ipairs(dirs) do
            lines[i].From = Vector2.new(cx + d[1] * gap, cy + d[2] * gap)
            lines[i].To = Vector2.new(cx + d[1] * (gap + arm), cy + d[2] * (gap + arm))
            lines[i].Visible = true
        end
    end

    local function targetHighlightLoop()
        local target = self:getTarget()
        for player, state in pairs(self.players) do
            if not state.character then continue end
            local highlight = state.highlight
            local shouldShow = target == player
            if shouldShow ~= state.visible then
                state.visible = shouldShow
                if highlight then
                    highlight.Enabled = shouldShow
                end
            end
        end
    end

    function self:getTarget()
        if not Toggles.target_highlight.Value then return nil end
        return self.targetPlayer
    end

    function self:setTarget(player)
        self.targetPlayer = player
    end

    local function onPlayerAdded(player)
        local state = {
            player = player,
            character = nil,
            highlight = nil,
            visible = false,
        }
        self.players[player] = state

        local function onCharacterAdded(character)
            state.character = character
            if state.highlight then
                state.highlight:Destroy()
            end
            local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
            highlight.Name = "TargetHighlight"
            highlight.FillColor = Options.target_highlight_fill.Value
            highlight.OutlineColor = Options.target_highlight_outline.Value
            highlight.FillTransparency = Options.target_highlight_fill.Value and 1 or 0.3
            highlight.OutlineTransparency = Options.target_highlight_outline.Value and 1 or 0
            highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            highlight.Enabled = false
            highlight.Parent = character
            state.highlight = highlight
        end

        player.CharacterAdded:Connect(onCharacterAdded)
        if player.Character then
            onCharacterAdded(player.Character)
        end
    end

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        onPlayerAdded(player)
    end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(onPlayerAdded)

    local function tracerRender()
        if not Toggles.target_tracer.Value then return end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local origin = cam.CFrame.Position
        local center = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(origin)

        for player, state in pairs(self.players) do
            if player == LocalPlayer then continue end
            local character = state.character
            if not character then continue end
            local hum = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then continue end
            local ally = character:FindFirstChild("_is_ally")
            if ally and ally.Value then continue end

            local hrp = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hrp then continue end

            local screen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position)
            local line = state.line
            if not line then
                line = Drawing.new("Line")
                line.Thickness = 1
                line.Color = Options.target_tracer_customization.Value or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                table.insert(self.drawings, line)
                state.line = line
            end
            line.From = Vector2.new(center.X, center.Y)
            line.To = Vector2.new(screen.X, screen.Y)
            line.Visible = true
        end
    end

    local function lightingLoop()
        if Toggles.skybox_enabled.Value then
            local value = Options.skybox_value.Value or "Heaven"
            local asset = "rbxassetid://1898729298"
            Lighting.SkyboxBk = asset
            Lighting.SkyboxBl = asset
            Lighting.SkyboxDn = asset
            Lighting.SkyboxFt = asset
            Lighting.SkyboxLf = asset
            Lighting.SkyboxRt = asset
            Lighting.SkyboxUp = asset
        end
    end

    local weatherEmitter = nil
    local weatherAttachment = nil

    local function weatherLoop()
        if not Toggles.weather_enabled.Value then return end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end

        local weatherType = Options.weather_value.Value or "Rain"
        if not weatherEmitter then
            weatherAttachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
            weatherAttachment.Name = "WeatherAttachment"
            weatherAttachment.Parent = cam
            weatherEmitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
            weatherEmitter.Name = "WeatherEmitter"
            weatherEmitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(3)
            weatherEmitter.Rate = 30
            weatherEmitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(10, 20)
            weatherEmitter.Size = NumberSequence.new(0.5)
            weatherEmitter.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(0)
            weatherEmitter.Parent = weatherAttachment
        end

        local color = Options.weather_color_1.Value or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        local lifetimeMultiplier = Options.weather_lifetime_multiplier.Value or 1
        local rateMultiplier = Options.weather_rate_multiplier.Value or 1
        local timescale = Options.weather_timescale_multiplier.Value or 1

        if weatherType == "Rain" then
            weatherEmitter.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/raindrop.dds"
            weatherEmitter.Color = ColorSequence.new(color)
            weatherEmitter.Rate = 40 * rateMultiplier
            weatherEmitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(25 * timescale, 40 * timescale)
            weatherEmitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(2 * lifetimeMultiplier)
        elseif weatherType == "Snow" then
            weatherEmitter.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/snow.dds"
            weatherEmitter.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
            weatherEmitter.Rate = 12 * rateMultiplier
            weatherEmitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(2 * timescale, 6 * timescale)
            weatherEmitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(4 * lifetimeMultiplier)
            weatherEmitter.Size = NumberSequence.new(0.8)
        elseif weatherType == "Fog" or weatherType == "Mist" then
            weatherEmitter.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke.dds"
            weatherEmitter.Color = ColorSequence.new(color)
            weatherEmitter.Rate = 8 * rateMultiplier
            weatherEmitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(1 * timescale, 3 * timescale)
            weatherEmitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(5 * lifetimeMultiplier)
            weatherEmitter.Size = NumberSequence.new(2)
        end
    end

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(crosshairRender)
    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(targetHighlightLoop)
    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(tracerRender)
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(lightingLoop)
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(weatherLoop)

    return self
end

function World:destroy()
    for _, drawing in ipairs(self.drawings) do
        pcall(function()
            drawing:Remove()
        end)
    end
    self.drawings = {}
end

-- ============================================================================

-- VIEWMODEL & ARMS

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local Viewmodel = {}
Viewmodel.__index = Viewmodel

function Viewmodel.new()
    local self = setmetatable({}, Viewmodel)
    self.cache = {}
    self.arms = {}
    self.highlights = {}
    self.animations = {}

    local function getViewModels()
        return workspace:FindFirstChild("viewmodels")
    end

    local function getEquippedItem(character)
        return character and character:FindFirstChild("EquippedItem")
    end

    local function getViewModel(item)
        return item and item.ViewModel
    end

    local function applyOffset(vm, dt)
        if not vm then return end
        local ox = Options.viewmodel_offset_x.Value
        local oy = Options.viewmodel_offset_y.Value
        local oz = Options.viewmodel_offset_z.Value
        vm.CFrame = vm.CFrame * CFrame.new(ox, oy, oz)
    end

    local function applyBodyAppearance(vm)
        if not vm then return end
        local color = Toggles.viewmodel_body_color.Value
        local transparency = Options.viewmodel_body_transparency.Value
        local material = Options.viewmodel_material.Value
        local override = Toggles.viewmodel_override_appearance.Value
        for _, desc in ipairs(vm:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("BasePart") then
                if color or override then
                    pcall(function()
                        desc.Color = Options.viewmodel_body_color_value.Value
                    end)
                end
                if transparency then
                    pcall(function()
                        desc.Transparency = transparency
                    end)
                end
                if material ~= "Default" then
                    pcall(function()
                        desc.Material = Enum.Material[material]
                    end)
                end
            elseif desc:IsA("Texture") or desc:IsA("Decal") then
                if Toggles.viewmodel_disable_textures.Value then
                    pcall(function()
                        desc.Texture = ""
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function applyWireframe(vm)
        if not vm then return end
        local wireframe = Toggles.viewmodel_wireframe.Value
        for _, desc in ipairs(vm:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("BasePart") then
                pcall(function()
                    desc.Material = wireframe and Enum.Material.ForceField or desc.Material
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    local function applyChams(vm)
        if not vm then return end
        local chams = Toggles.viewmodel_chams.Value
        local highlight = vm:FindFirstChild("VMChams")
        if not chams then
            if highlight then highlight:Destroy() end
            return
        end
        if not highlight then
            highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
            highlight.Name = "VMChams"
            highlight.FillColor = Options.viewmodel_chams_color.Value
            highlight.OutlineColor = Options.viewmodel_chams_color2.Value or Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
            highlight.FillTransparency = Options.viewmodel_chams_alpha_fill.Value
            highlight.OutlineTransparency = Options.viewmodel_chams_alpha_outline.Value
            highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            highlight.Parent = vm
        end
    end

    local function applyRecoil(vm, dt)
        if not vm then return end
        local recoil = Options.viewmodel_recoil.Value
        if recoil and recoil > 0 then
            local rot = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-recoil), 0, math.rad(recoil * 0.5))
            vm.CFrame = vm.CFrame * rot
        end
    end

    local function handleFramesOverride(vm)
        if not Toggles.viewmodel_frames_override.Value then return end
        local value = Options.viewmodel_frames_value.Value
        for _, desc in ipairs(vm:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("MeshPart") then
                pcall(function()
                    desc.MeshId = value
                end)
            elseif desc:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
                pcall(function()
                    desc.MeshId = value
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    local function update()
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not character then return end
        local item = getEquippedItem(character)
        if not item then return end
        local vm = getViewModel(item)
        if not vm then return end

        local settings = Options.viewmodel_options.Value or {}
        applyOffset(vm)
        applyBodyAppearance(vm)
        applyWireframe(vm)
        applyChams(vm)
        applyRecoil(vm)
        handleFramesOverride(vm)
    end

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(update)

    return self
end

function Viewmodel:setArmsData(character, data)
    -- proto 763 SetArmsData: apply arm customization (arm body color/material)
    local leftArm = character and character:FindFirstChild("LeftArm")
    local rightArm = character and character:FindFirstChild("RightArm")
    if not leftArm or not rightArm then return end

    if Toggles.arm_body_color.Value then
        local color = Options.arm_body_color_value.Value
        pcall(function()
            leftArm.Color = color
            rightArm.Color = color
        end)
    end
    if Options.arm_material.Value ~= "Default" then
        pcall(function()
            local mat = Enum.Material[Options.arm_material.Value]
            leftArm.Material = mat
            rightArm.Material = mat
        end)
    end
    if Options.arm_body_transparency.Value then
        pcall(function()
            leftArm.Transparency = Options.arm_body_transparency.Value
            rightArm.Transparency = Options.arm_body_transparency.Value
        end)
    end
    if Toggles.arm_disable_textures.Value then
        for _, child in ipairs(leftArm:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Texture") or child:IsA("Decal") then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(rightArm:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Texture") or child:IsA("Decal") then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
end

function Viewmodel:applyAnimationTrack(item, name, speed)
    -- proto 200: GetAnimationTrack + AdjustSpeed (idle/aiming/bobbing/equip/jump/sprint/slide)
    local vm = item and item.ViewModel
    if not vm then return end
    local animator = vm:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not animator then return end
    local track = animator:GetAnimationTrack(animator:LoadAnimation(Instance.new("Animation")))
    if track then
        track:AdjustSpeed(speed or Options.animation_player_speed.Value)
        track:Play()
    end
end

-- ============================================================================

-- COSMETIC CHANGER & WEAPON FILTER

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local Cosmetics = {}
Cosmetics.__index = Cosmetics

function Cosmetics.new()
    local self = setmetatable({}, Cosmetics)
    self.cache = {}
    self.skins = {}
    self.charms = {}
    self.finishers = {}
    self.ownableWeapons = {}

    local function getEquippedItem(character)
        return character and character:FindFirstChild("EquippedItem")
    end

    local function weaponOf(item)
        return item and item.Info and item.Info.Name or (item and item.Name)
    end

    local function itemIndex(item)
        return item and item.Data and item.Data.ItemIndex
    end

    function self:loadSaveData()
        -- proto 278/285: cosmetics_changer_save_data (JSON)
        local data = Options.cosmetics_changer_save_data.Value
        if not data or data == "" then return end
        local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(data)
        end)
        if ok and decoded then
            self.skins = decoded.skins or {}
            self.charms = decoded.charms or {}
            self.finishers = decoded.finishers or {}
        end
    end

    function self:saveData()
        local payload = HttpService:JSONEncode({
            skins = self.skins,
            charms = self.charms,
            finishers = self.finishers,
        })
        Options.cosmetics_changer_save_data:SetValue(payload)
    end

    function self:applySkin(character, skinName)
        local item = getEquippedItem(character)
        if not item then return end
        local name = weaponOf(item)
        local skin = self.skins[name] or skinName
        if not skin or skin == "none" then return end

        local handle = item:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if handle then
            pcall(function()
                handle.TextureID = skin
            end)
        end
        for _, desc in ipairs(item:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("Texture") then
                pcall(function()
                    desc.Texture = skin
                end)
            elseif desc:IsA("SpecialMesh") or desc:IsA("MeshPart") then
                pcall(function()
                    desc.TextureId = skin
                end)
            end
        end
        Library:Notify("applied " .. (skinName or skin) .. " to " .. name, 2)
    end

    function self:applyCharm(character, charmName)
        if not charmName or charmName == "none" then return end
        local item = getEquippedItem(character)
        if not item then return end
        local charm = self.charms[charmName]
        if not charm then return end
        local handle = item:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if not handle then return end
        local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
        weld.Part0 = handle
        weld.Part1 = charm
        weld.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 1, 0)
        weld.Parent = handle
    end

    function self:applyFinisher(character, finisherName)
        if not finisherName or finisherName == "none" then return end
        self.finishers[finisherName] = true
    end

    function self:applyWrap(character, wrapName, inverted)
        if not wrapName or wrapName == "none" then return end
        local item = getEquippedItem(character)
        if not item then return end
        for _, desc in ipairs(item:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("Texture") or desc:IsA("Decal") then
                pcall(function()
                    desc.Texture = wrapName
                    if inverted then
                        desc.StudsPerTileU = -math.abs(desc.StudsPerTileU or 1)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    function self:randomSkin()
        local names = {}
        for name in pairs(self.skins) do
            table.insert(names, name)
        end
        if #names == 0 then return "none" end
        return names[math.random(#names)]
    end

    function self:randomFinisher()
        local names = {}
        for name in pairs(self.finishers) do
            table.insert(names, name)
        end
        if #names == 0 then return "none" end
        return names[math.random(#names)]
    end

    -- weapon filter (proto 272/280)
    function self:setWeaponFilter(weapon, enabled)
        local filter = Options.weapon_filter.Value or {}
        filter[weapon] = enabled
        Options.weapon_filter:SetValue(filter)
    end

    function self:isWeaponFiltered(weapon)
        local filter = Options.weapon_filter.Value or {}
        return filter[weapon] == false
    end

    self:loadSaveData()

    return self
end

function Cosmetics:destroy()
    self.cache = {}
    self.skins = {}
    self.charms = {}
    self.finishers = {}
end

-- ============================================================================

-- CAMERA SYSTEM

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local Camera = {}
Camera.__index = Camera

function Camera.new()
    local self = setmetatable({}, Camera)
    self.cameraData = {}

    local function updateFov()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end
        if Toggles.camera_fov_changer.Value then
            cam.FieldOfView = Options.camera_fov_changer_amount.Value
        end
    end

    local function updateAspectRatio()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end
        if Toggles.camera_aspect_ratio.Value then
            cam.AspectRatio = Options.camera_aspect_ratio_x.Value / Options.camera_aspect_ratio_y.Value
        end
    end

    local function antiFlashbang()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end
        if Toggles.camera_anti_flashbang.Value then
            pcall(function()
                cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
                cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(cam.CFrame.Position, cam.CFrame.Position + cam.CFrame.LookVector)
            end)
        end
    end

    local function cameraBlur()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end
        local blur = Options.camera_blur.Value
        if blur and blur > 0 then
            local effect = workspace.CurrentCamera:FindFirstChild("CameraBlur")
            if not effect then
                effect = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
                effect.Name = "CameraBlur"
                effect.Parent = cam
            end
            effect.Size = blur
        else
            local effect = cam:FindFirstChild("CameraBlur")
            if effect then effect:Destroy() end
        end
    end

    -- camera data system (proto 480/481/499/478)
    function self:getCameraData(character)
        return self.cameraData[character]
    end

    function self:setCameraData(character, data)
        self.cameraData[character] = data
    end

    function self:getCameraCFrame(character)
        local data = self.cameraData[character]
        return data and data.CameraCFrame or (character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
    end
