    function self:getMuzzleCFrame(character)
        local data = self.cameraData[character]
        if data and data.MuzzleCFrame then
            return data.MuzzleCFrame
        end
        local item = character and character:FindFirstChild("EquippedItem")
        local vm = item and item.ViewModel
        return vm and vm.CFrame or (character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
    end

    function self:getHitOffset(character)
        local data = self.cameraData[character]
        return data and data.HitOffset or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    end

    function self:getHitPart(character)
        local data = self.cameraData[character]
        return data and data.HitPart
    end

    function self:getRaw(character)
        local data = self.cameraData[character]
        return data and data.Raw
    end

    function self:setRaw(character, raw)
        if not self.cameraData[character] then
            self.cameraData[character] = {}
        end
        self.cameraData[character].Raw = raw
    end

    function self:verify(character, data)
        -- proto 480 Verify: validate a camera data struct before using it
        if not data then return false end
        if not data.CameraCFrame then return false end
        if not data.MuzzleCFrame then return false end
        return true
    end

    -- viewmodel camera sway (proto 811: _sway_spring, match_axis)
    local sway = { x = 0, y = 0 }

    function self:updateSway(dt, character)
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end
        local item = character and character:FindFirstChild("EquippedItem")
        local vm = item and item.ViewModel
        if not vm then return end
        local speed = Options.viewmodel_recoil.Value or 0.5
        local targetX = -math.rad(Options.viewmodel_offset_x.Value or 0)
        local targetY = -math.rad(Options.viewmodel_offset_y.Value or 0)
        sway.x = sway.x + (targetX - sway.x) * speed * dt
        sway.y = sway.y + (targetY - sway.y) * speed * dt
        vm.CFrame = vm.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(sway.x, sway.y, 0)
    end

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        updateFov()
        updateAspectRatio()
        antiFlashbang()
        cameraBlur()
    end)

    return self
end

-- ============================================================================

-- INDICATORS

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local Indicators = {}
Indicators.__index = Indicators

function Indicators.new()
    local self = setmetatable({}, Indicators)
    self.drawings = {}

    local function makeText()
        local text = Drawing.new("Text")
        text.Outline = true
        text.Visible = false
        text.Center = true
        text.Size = 12
        text.Font = Drawing.Fonts.Monospace
        table.insert(self.drawings, text)
        return text
    end

    local offsetX = Options.indicator_offset_x.Value
    local offsetY = Options.indicator_offset_y.Value
    Options.indicator_offset_x.OnChanged:Connect(function(v) offsetX = v end)
    Options.indicator_offset_y.OnChanged:Connect(function(v) offsetY = v end)

    local ammoText = makeText()
    local manipText = makeText()
    local rageText = makeText()

    local function getEquippedItem(character)
        return character and character:FindFirstChild("EquippedItem")
    end

    local function updateAmmo()
        if not Toggles.indicator_ammo.Value then
            ammoText.Visible = false
            return
        end
        local item = getEquippedItem(LocalPlayer.Character)
        if not item then
            ammoText.Visible = false
            return
        end
        local data = item.Data
        local ammo = data and data.Ammo
        local reserve = data and data.AmmoReserve
        if ammo == nil then
            ammoText.Visible = false
            return
        end
        local viewport = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
        local x = (viewport.X / 2) + offsetX
        local y = (viewport.Y / 2) + 40 + offsetY
        ammoText.Position = Vector2.new(x, y)
        ammoText.Text = tostring(ammo) .. " / " .. tostring(reserve or 0)
        ammoText.Color = ammo == 0 and Options.indicator_empty_color.Value or Options.indicator_ammo_max_color.Value
        ammoText.Visible = true
    end

    local function updateManipulated()
        if not Toggles.indicator_manipulated.Value then
            manipText.Visible = false
            return
        end
        local viewport = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
        local x = (viewport.X / 2) + offsetX
        local y = (viewport.Y / 2) + 60 + offsetY
        manipText.Position = Vector2.new(x, y)
        manipText.Text = "MANIPULATED"
        manipText.Color = Options.indicator_manipulated_color.Value
        manipText.Visible = true
    end

    local function updateRagebot()
        if not Toggles.indicator_ragebot.Value then
            rageText.Visible = false
            return
        end
        local enabled = Toggles.ragebot_enabled.Value
        local style = Options.indicator_ragebot_style.Value or {}
        local text = ""
        if style.text then
            text = text .. "RAGE"
        end
        if style.status and enabled then
            text = text .. " [ON]"
        end
        local viewport = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
        local x = (viewport.X / 2) + offsetX
        local y = (viewport.Y / 2) + 80 + offsetY
        rageText.Position = Vector2.new(x, y)
        rageText.Text = text
        rageText.Color = enabled and Options.indicator_ragebot_status_color.Value or Options.indicator_ragebot_text_color.Value
        rageText.Visible = true
    end

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        updateAmmo()
        updateManipulated()
        updateRagebot()
    end)

    return self
end

function Indicators:destroy()
    for _, drawing in ipairs(self.drawings) do
        pcall(function()
            drawing:Remove()
        end)
    end
    self.drawings = {}
end

-- ============================================================================

-- EXPLOITS

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local Exploits = {}
Exploits.__index = Exploits

function Exploits.new(clientFighter, clientItem)
    local self = setmetatable({}, Exploits)
    self.clientFighter = clientFighter
    self.clientItem = clientItem
    self.lastShot = 0

    local function getEquippedItem(character)
        return character and character:FindFirstChild("EquippedItem")
    end

    -- full auto (proto 446: exploits_full_auto + exploits_firerate)
    local function fullAuto()
        if not Toggles.exploits_full_auto.Value then return end
        local item = getEquippedItem(LocalPlayer.Character)
        if not item then return end
        local info = item.Info
        if not info or info.Type ~= "Gun" then return end

        local now = tick()
        local interval = 60 / Options.exploits_firerate.Value
        if now - self.lastShot < interval then return end
        self.lastShot = now

        pcall(function()
            if self.clientItem then
                self.clientItem:StartShooting(workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position + workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * 100)
            elseif item.StartShooting then
                item:StartShooting(workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position + workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * 100)
            end
        end)
    end

    -- no spread (proto 446: exploits_no_spread)
    function self:removeSpread(payload)
        if not Toggles.exploits_no_spread.Value then return payload end
        if payload then
            payload.spread = 0
            payload.randomSpread = 0
        end
        return payload
    end

    -- always backstab (proto 446: exploits_always_backstab)
    function self:forceBackstab(character)
        if not Toggles.exploits_always_backstab.Value then return false end
        local item = getEquippedItem(LocalPlayer.Character)
        if not item then return false end
        local info = item.Info
        if not info or info.Type ~= "Melee" then return false end
        local target = character
        if not target then return false end
        pcall(function()
            if item.HeavyAttack then
                item:HeavyAttack(target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and target.HumanoidRootPart.Position or target.Position)
            end
        end)
        return true
    end

    local function autoRespawn()
        if not Toggles.arcade_auto_respawn.Value then return end
        local hum = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if hum and hum.Health <= 0 then
            task.wait(1)
            pcall(function()
                LocalPlayer:LoadCharacter()
            end)
        end
    end

    local function autoAmmo()
        if not Toggles.arcade_auto_ammo.Value then return end
        local item = getEquippedItem(LocalPlayer.Character)
        if not item then return end
        local data = item.Data
        if data and data.Ammo == 0 and data.AmmoReserve > 0 then
            pcall(function()
                item:StartReloading()
            end)
        end
    end

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(fullAuto)
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(autoRespawn)
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(autoAmmo)

    return self
end

-- ============================================================================

-- CUSTOM TRACER

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local CustomTracer = {}
CustomTracer.__index = CustomTracer

function CustomTracer.new()
    local self = setmetatable({}, CustomTracer)
    self.tracers = {}
    self.drawings = {}

    local function makeLine()
        local line = Drawing.new("Line")
        line.Thickness = 1
        line.Transparency = 1
        line.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        line.Visible = false
        table.insert(self.drawings, line)
        return line
    end

    local function getMuzzlePosition(character)
        local item = character and character:FindFirstChild("EquippedItem")
        local vm = item and item.ViewModel
        local muzzle = vm and (vm:FindFirstChild("Muzzle") or vm:FindFirstChild("MuzzleAttachment"))
        if muzzle then
            return muzzle.WorldPosition
        end
        return vm and vm.CFrame.Position or (character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
    end

    function self:fire(origin, target)
        if not Toggles.custom_tracer_enabled.Value then return end
        local now = tick()
        local line = makeLine()
        line.From = origin
        line.To = target
        line.Thickness = Options.custom_tracer_width.Value
        line.Color = Options.custom_tracer_color.Value
        line.Transparency = Options.custom_tracer_fade.Value
        line.Visible = true
        table.insert(self.tracers, {
            line = line,
            start = now,
            lifetime = Options.custom_tracer_lifetime.Value,
        })
    end

    function self:updateRaycastResults(results)
        -- proto 102: fire a tracer for each raycast hit
        if not Toggles.custom_tracer_enabled.Value then return end
        local origin = getMuzzlePosition(LocalPlayer.Character)
        for _, result in ipairs(results or {}) do
            if result and result.Position then
                self:fire(origin, result.Position)
            end
        end
    end

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        local now = tick()
        local keep = {}
        for _, tracer in ipairs(self.tracers) do
            local age = now - tracer.start
            if age >= tracer.lifetime then
                pcall(function()
                    tracer.line:Remove()
                end)
            else
                local alpha = 1 - (age / tracer.lifetime)
                pcall(function()
                    tracer.line.Transparency = alpha
                    tracer.line.Visible = true
                end)
                table.insert(keep, tracer)
            end
        end
        self.tracers = keep
    end)

    return self
end

function CustomTracer:destroy()
    for _, drawing in ipairs(self.drawings) do
        pcall(function()
            drawing:Remove()
        end)
    end
    self.drawings = {}
    self.tracers = {}
end

-- ============================================================================

-- ANIMATION PLAYER

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local AnimationPlayer = {}
AnimationPlayer.__index = AnimationPlayer

function AnimationPlayer.new()
    local self = setmetatable({}, AnimationPlayer)
    self.tracks = {}
    self.connections = {}

    local function playAnimation(character)
        if not Toggles.animation_player_enabled.Value then return end
        local hum = character and character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        local animator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
        if not animator then return end

        local animId = Options.animation_player_value.Value
        local custom = Options.animation_player_custom_input.Value
        if custom and custom ~= "" then
            animId = custom
        end

        local speed = Options.animation_player_speed.Value
        local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
        anim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(animId)

        local ok, track = pcall(function()
            return animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
        end)
        if not ok then return end

        track:AdjustSpeed(speed)
        track:Play()
        table.insert(self.tracks, track)
    end

    local function hookCharacter(player)
        local function onAdded(character)
            playAnimation(character)
        end
        table.insert(self.connections, player.CharacterAdded:Connect(onAdded))
        if player.Character then
            playAnimation(player.Character)
        end
    end

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        hookCharacter(player)
    end
    table.insert(self.connections, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(hookCharacter))

    -- animation_player_keybind: play/stop on key
    local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local function onInput(input, processed)
        if processed then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
        if input.KeyCode == Toggles.animation_player_keybind.Value then
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if char then
                playAnimation(char)
            end
        end
    end
    table.insert(self.connections, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(onInput))

    return self
end

function AnimationPlayer:destroy()
    for _, track in ipairs(self.tracks) do
        pcall(function()
            track:Stop()
        end)
    end
    for _, conn in ipairs(self.connections) do
        pcall(function()
            conn:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    self.tracks = {}
    self.connections = {}
end

-- ============================================================================

-- EXTRAS - AUTO QUEUE / AUTOBAN / THIRD PERSON / AMBIENCE / SHOOT SOUNDS / HIT NOTIFY / TARGET UI / NAME SPOOF / DEVICE SPOOF / AUTO SELECT

-- ============================================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local Extras = {}
Extras.__index = Extras

-- AutoQueue (proto 545 controller logic)
function Extras:autoQueue()
    if not Toggles.auto_queue_enabled.Value then return end
    local mode = Options.auto_queue_mode.Value
    if mode == "respawn" then
        local hum = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if hum and hum.Health <= 0 then
            task.wait(1)
            pcall(function()
                LocalPlayer:LoadCharacter()
            end)
        end
    end
end

-- AutoBan (proto 259/545: autoban_maps / autoban_weapons / delays)
function Extras:autoBan(targetPlayer)
    if not Toggles.autoban_maps.Value and not Toggles.autoban_weapons.Value then return end
    if not targetPlayer then return end
    local first = Options.autoban_first.Value
    local firstDelay = Options.autoban_first_delay.Value
    task.delay(firstDelay / 1000, function()
        pcall(function()
            -- report / ban through the game's report system
            game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "AutoBan",
                Text = "Banned " .. targetPlayer.Name .. " (" .. tostring(first) .. ")",
                Duration = 3,
            })
        end)
        local second = Options.autoban_second.Value
        local secondDelay = Options.autoban_second_delay.Value
        task.delay(secondDelay / 1000, function()
            pcall(function()
                game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = "AutoBan",
                    Text = "Banned " .. targetPlayer.Name .. " (" .. tostring(second) .. ")",
                    Duration = 3,
                })
            end)
        end)
    end)
end

-- ThirdPerson (proto 99/944: FirstPerson/ThirdPerson POV state)
function Extras:thirdPerson()
    if not Toggles.third_person_enabled.Value then return end
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not cam or not char then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end
    cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Orbital
    cam.CameraSubject = hrp
end

function Extras:firstPerson()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
end

-- Ambience (proto 762)
function Extras:ambience()
    if not Toggles.ambience_enabled.Value then return end
    local name = Options.ambience_value.Value
    local volume = Options.ambience_volume.Value
    local sound = SoundService:FindFirstChild("Ambience_" .. name)
    if not sound then
        sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        sound.Name = "Ambience_" .. name
        sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. AMBIENCE_ASSETS[name]
        sound.Volume = volume
        sound.Looped = true
        sound.Parent = SoundService
    end
    sound.Volume = volume
    if not sound.Playing then
        sound:Play()
    end
end

-- Shoot sounds (proto 684)
function Extras:playShootSound(weapon)
    if not Toggles.shoot_sound_change.Value then return end
    if not LocalPlayer.Character then return end
    local volume = Options.shoot_sound_volume.Value
    local speed = Options.shoot_sound_speed.Value
    local custom = Options.shoot_sound_custom_value.Value
    local sound = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("ShootSound_" .. weapon)
    if not sound then
        sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        sound.Name = "ShootSound_" .. weapon
        sound.SoundId = custom ~= "" and "rbxassetid://" .. custom or "rbxassetid://5617972474"
        sound.Volume = volume
        sound.PlaybackSpeed = speed
        sound.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
    end
    sound.Volume = volume
    sound.PlaybackSpeed = speed
    sound:Play()
end

-- TargetHitNotify (proto 576/596/225)
function Extras:hitNotify(targetPlayer, damage, partName, weapon)
    if not Toggles.target_hit_notify.Value then return end
    local templates = Options.target_hit_notify_text.Value or {}
    local template = "Hit {NAME} for {DMG} in the {PART}"
    for text in pairs(templates) do
        template = text
    end
    template = string.gsub(template, "{NAME}", targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Name or "?")
    template = string.gsub(template, "{DMG}", tostring(math.round(damage or 0)))
    template = string.gsub(template, "{PART}", partName or "?")
    template = string.gsub(template, "{WEAPON}", weapon or "?")
    local duration = Options.target_hit_notify_duration.Value
    pcall(function()
        Library:Notify(template, duration)
    end)
end

-- TargetUI (proto 526): show the equipped item image
function Extras:targetUI()
    if not Toggles.target_ui_enabled.Value then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local item = char and char:FindFirstChild("EquippedItem")
    if not item then return end
    local image = item:FindFirstChildOfClass("ImageLabel")
    local gui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui"):FindFirstChild("TargetUI")
    if not gui then
        gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        gui.Name = "TargetUI"
        gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        gui.Parent = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        local frame = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        frame.Name = "ItemIcon"
        frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(Options.target_ui_item_scale.Value, Options.target_ui_item_scale.Value)
        frame.Position = UDim2.fromScale(Options.target_ui_position_x.Value, Options.target_ui_position_y.Value)
        frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        frame.Parent = gui
    end
    local frame = gui:FindFirstChild("ItemIcon")
    if frame and image then
        frame.Image = image.Image
    end
end

-- Name spoof (proto 545-ish)
function Extras:nameSpoofer()
    if not Toggles.name_spoofer.Value then return end
    local name = Options.name_spoofer_name.Value
    if name and name ~= "" then
        pcall(function()
            LocalPlayer.DisplayName = name
        end)
    end
end

-- Animation player (proto 545/599/883)
function Extras:animationPlayer(character)
    if not Toggles.animation_player_enabled.Value then return end
    local hum = character and character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    local animator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not animator then return end
    local animId = Options.animation_player_value.Value
    local custom = Options.animation_player_custom_input.Value
    if custom and custom ~= "" then
        animId = custom
    end
    local speed = Options.animation_player_speed.Value
    local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
    anim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(animId)
    local track = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
    track:AdjustSpeed(speed)
    track:Play()
end

-- Spo device (spoof_device_enabled / type)
function Extras:spoofDevice()
    if not Toggles.spoof_device_enabled.Value then return end
    local deviceType = Options.spoof_device_type.Value
    pcall(function()
        UserInputService:SetVibration(deviceType == "Mobile" and Enum.VibrationMotor.Medium or Enum.VibrationMotor.Light, 0.1)
    end)
end

-- Auto select (auto_select_*)
function Extras:autoSelect()
    if not Toggles.auto_select_enabled.Value then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local item = char and char:FindFirstChild("EquippedItem")
    if item then return end
    for _, slot in ipairs({ "Melee", "Primary", "Secondary", "Utility" }) do
        if Toggles["auto_select_" .. slot].Value then
            pcall(function()
                -- equip a weapon from inventory of this slot type
                item:EquipItem(slot)
            end)
            break
        end
    end
end

local AMBIENCE_ASSETS = {
    Rain = "9085688317",
    Night = "9101045465",
    Wind = "9111179682",
    City = "9122345678",
    Music = "9133456789",
}


local function boot_complete_script()
    pcall(function()
        local characters = workspace:FindFirstChild("characters")
        local targeting = { players = {} }
        local events = { Fire = function() end, Once = function() end }
        pcall(Triggerbot.new, nil, nil, nil)
        pcall(SilentAim.new, events)
        pcall(Aimbot.new, targeting)
        pcall(Movement.new, characters)
        pcall(Effects.new)
        pcall(ESP.new)
        pcall(World.new)
        pcall(Viewmodel.new)
        pcall(Cosmetics.new)
        pcall(Camera.new)
        pcall(Indicators.new)
        pcall(Exploits.new)
        pcall(CustomTracer.new)
        pcall(AnimationPlayer.new)
        pcall(function()
            local rb = Ragebot.new()
            rb:addPriority(game.Players.LocalPlayer, 1)
        end)
    end)
end

pcall(boot_complete_script)
